## Codebase Overview: YourFamilyFarmer

This is a **Next.js 16 + React 19 + TypeScript** application connecting farmers with consumers for direct produce sales. It uses **Supabase** as the backend database and supports **English + Telugu** localization.

---

## Directory Structure

```
/src
├── /app                          # Next.js App Router
│   ├── layout.tsx               # Root layout with LanguageProvider
│   ├── page.tsx                 # Home page
│   ├── /api/produce
│   │   ├── route.ts            # GET /api/produce - fetch available produce
│   │   └── search/route.ts      # GET /api/produce/search - search & filter
│   ├── /farmer
│   │   ├── page.tsx            # Farmer hub landing
│   │   ├── [slug]/page.tsx      # Farmer profile page (PUBLIC)
│   │   ├── login/page.tsx       # Farmer phone login
│   │   └── dashboard/page.tsx   # Farmer dashboard (PRIVATE)
│   ├── /consumer/page.tsx       # Consumer home (browses produce)
│   ├── /region/[slug]/page.tsx  # Region-specific page
│   └── globals.css              # Tailwind CSS
├── /components
│   ├── LanguageToggle.tsx       # EN/TE language switcher
│   ├── /consumer
│   │   ├── Cart.tsx            # Shopping cart + WhatsApp ordering
│   │   └── GlobalNav.tsx        # Top navigation
│   ├── /farmer
│   │   ├── FarmCover.tsx        # Hero section with farm photo
│   │   ├── StickyBottomBar.tsx  # CTA buttons (WhatsApp + Shop)
│   │   ├── TabSection.tsx       # Tab navigation
│   │   ├── TopNav.tsx           # Header with back button
│   │   ├── TrustStrip.tsx       # Stats badge strip
│   │   └── /tabs
│   │       ├── StoryTab.tsx     # Farmer story + visit CTA
│   │       ├── ProduceTab.tsx   # Product listing (edit mode)
│   │       ├── QualityTab.tsx   # Quality metrics
│   │       ├── ReviewsTab.tsx   # Customer reviews
│   │       └── FarmMediaTab.tsx # Photo gallery
│   └── /region
│       ├── RegionContent.tsx
│       ├── RegionHero.tsx
│       ├── RegionMap.tsx
│       ├── RegionTopBar.tsx
│       └── /tabs
│           ├── AddFarmerTab.tsx
│           ├── BrowseProduceTab.tsx
│           └── FarmersTab.tsx
└── /lib
    ├── supabase.ts             # Supabase client
    ├── LanguageContext.tsx     # i18n context provider
    └── translations.ts         # EN/TE strings
```

## Key Files Content

### 1. **Supabase Client** (`/src/lib/supabase.ts`)
```typescript
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'

const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!
const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!

export const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey)
```
Uses environment variables: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`.

---

### 2. **Farmer Profile Page** (`/src/app/farmer/[slug]/page.tsx`)

**Key features:**
- Fetches farmer by slug from `farmers` table
- Loads produce listings from `produce_listings` table
- Fetches reviews (approved only) from `reviews` table
- Loads farm media from `media` table
- Server-side rendering with async metadata generation
- Returns 404 if farmer not found or inactive

**Data flow:**
1. Fetch farmer info (id, name, village, district, story_quote)
2. Fetch all produce by farmer_id
3. Fetch approved reviews by farmer_id
4. Fetch media ordered by sort_order
5. Pass data to child components (tabs, sticky bar)

**URL:** `/farmer/[slug]` (e.g., `/farmer/john-doe-abc`)

---

### 3. **Cart Logic** (`/src/components/consumer/Cart.tsx`)

**Types:**
```typescript
type CartItem = {
  listingId: string
  qty: number
  name: string
  variety?: string
  emoji?: string
  pricePerKg?: number
  priceTier1Qty?: number
  priceTier1Price?: number
  priceTier2Qty?: number
  priceTier2Price?: number
  priceTier3Price?: number
  farmerId: string
  farmerName: string
  farmerPhone: string
  farmerVillage: string
  farmerSlug: string
  farmerPickupLocations?: string[]
}

type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
```

**Storage:**
- **Cart:** `localStorage.yff_cart_v1` (JSON-encoded)
- **Consumer info:** `localStorage.yff_consumer_v1` (name + phone)
- **Event:** `yff:cart-change` dispatched on updates

**Hooks:**
- `useCart()` - returns: `{ cart, items, count, addItem, setQty, removeItem, clear }`
- `useConsumerInfo()` - returns: `{ info, save }`

**Tier Logic:**
```typescript
function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem) {
  // Returns active price tier based on quantity
  // Tier 1: <= tier1_qty @ tier1_price
  // Tier 2: <= tier2_qty @ tier2_price (discounted)
  // Tier 3: > tier2_qty @ tier3_price
}
```

**WhatsApp Ordering (`handleOrderFarmer`):**
- Groups cart items by farmer
- Builds message with: emoji + name + variety + qty + price
- Appends consumer name & phone
- Appends pickup location if selected
- Opens WhatsApp: `wa.me/{farmerPhone}?text={message}`
- Marks farmer as "sent" in UI

---

### 4. **WhatsApp Send Button Handler** (`/src/components/consumer/Cart.tsx` lines 187-217)

```typescript
const handleOrderFarmer = (group: CartItem[]) => {
  if (detailsMissing) return
  saveInfo({ name: name.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })

  const f = group[0]
  const lines = group.map((it) => {
    const price = it.pricePerKg ? ` @ ₹${it.pricePerKg}/kg` : ''
    return `• ${it.emoji ?? '🌿'} ${it.name}${it.variety ? ` (${it.variety})` : ''} — ${it.qty} kg${price}`
  })

  const selectedPickup = pickupByFarmer[f.farmerId]
  const pickupLine = selectedPickup
    ? `\n\n*Pickup location / పికప్ స్థలం:* ${selectedPickup} (${f.farmerVillage})`
    : `\n\n*Pickup from your farm / మీ పొలం నుండి పికప్* (${f.farmerVillage})`

  const msg =
    `Hello ${f.farmerName} anna! 🙏\n` +
    `I saw your produce on YourFamilyFarmer and would like to order:\n\n` +
    lines.join('\n') +
    pickupLine +
    `\n\nMy name / నా పేరు: ${name.trim()}\n` +
    `My WhatsApp / నా వాట్సాప్: +91 ${phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)}\n\n` +
    `Please share a good pickup time. Thank you! / పికప్ సమయం తెలియజేయండి, ధన్యవాదాలు 🌱`

  const digits = f.farmerPhone.replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '')
  const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits
  const waUrl = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`

  window.open(waUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')
  setSentFarmers((s) => ({ ...s, [f.farmerId]: true }))
}
```

**Key details:**
- Validates consumer name + phone (minimum 10 digits)
- Groups items by farmer
- Constructs bilingual message (English + Telugu)
- Extracts 10-digit phone, formats as 91XXXXXXXXXX
- Opens WhatsApp with pre-filled message
- Tracks sent state per farmer

---

### 5. **Farmer Dashboard** (`/src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx`)

**Private page** (requires `yff_farmer_id` in localStorage).

**Data loaded:**
1. Farmer profile from `farmers` table
2. Active listings count (status='available')
3. Orders count (last 7 days)
4. Demand intents (unfulfilled, region-specific)

**Features:**
- **Stats cards:** Active listings, orders this week, avg rating, total buyers
- **Demand chart:** Top 5 unfulfilled demand intents by crop
- **Add produce modal:** Full produce listing form
- **Manage listings modal:** View all listings, delete button per listing
- **Profile edit modal:** Edit name, village, district, method, farming_since_year, pickup_locations

**Modals:**

1. **ProfileEditModal:**
   - Editable fields: name, village, district, farming method, farming_since_year
   - Pickup locations (add/remove tags)
   - Auto-generates slug from name if currently auto-generated pattern (`f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}`)
   - Updates farmer row via Supabase

2. **ProduceListingForm:**
   - Emoji picker
   - Name + variety
   - Qty + availability period
   - Farming method dropdown
   - Pricing tiers (tier 1, tier 2, tier 3)
   - Description (500 char max)
   - Image upload (to `farm-images` storage bucket)
   - Quality params (BRIX, Soil Organic Carbon %)
   - Inserts to `produce_listings` table

3. **ManageListingsModal:**
   - Lists all produce for farmer
   - Status badge (available/coming_soon)
   - Delete with confirmation
   - Shows image, name, variety, price, stock, method

---

### 6. **Active Listings Management** (Same file, lines 1055-1243)

**ManageListingsModal** fetches & displays:
```typescript
SELECT id, name, variety, emoji, status, method, stock_qty, price_tier_1_price, image_url, created_at
FROM produce_listings
WHERE farmer_id = ?
ORDER BY created_at DESC
```

**Delete flow:**
- Confirmation dialog with produce name
- DELETE from produce_listings
- Reload dashboard stats

---

### 7. **Produce Tab** (`/src/components/farmer/tabs/ProduceTab.tsx`)

**Displays:**
- Pickup location selector (if farmer has multiple)
- Available produce list
- Coming soon list
- Add produce form (if `isEditMode=true`)

**ProduceCard component:**
- Shows emoji/image, name, variety, method, price tiers
- Stock qty badge
- WhatsApp order link
- Delete button (in edit mode)

**ComingSoonCard:**
- Dashed border design
- Notify me form (name + phone)
- Expected date display
- Inserts to `notify_requests` table

## Supabase Tables (Inferred)

### `farmers`
- `id` (uuid)
- `name` (text)
- `phone` (text - stored as digits)
- `slug` (text, unique)
- `village` (text)
- `district` (text)
- `method` (text: natural/organic/low_chemical/chemical)
- `region_slug` (text)
- `active` (boolean)
- `rating_avg` (numeric)
- `buyer_count` (integer)
- `farming_since_year` (integer)
- `pickup_locations` (text[] or JSON)
- `story_quote` (text)
- `farm_size_acres` (numeric)
- `water_source` (text)
- `farm_visit_day` (text)
- `pickup_available` (boolean)
- `delivery_available` (boolean)

### `produce_listings`
- `id` (uuid)
- `farmer_id` (uuid, FK)
- `name` (text)
- `variety` (text, nullable)
- `emoji` (text)
- `status` (text: available/coming_soon)
- `method` (text)
- `price_tier_1_qty` (integer)
- `price_tier_1_price` (numeric)
- `price_tier_2_qty` (integer)
- `price_tier_2_price` (numeric)
- `price_tier_3_qty` (integer)
- `price_tier_3_price` (numeric)
- `stock_qty` (numeric)
- `description` (text)
- `image_url` (text)
- `brix` (numeric)
- `soil_organic_carbon` (numeric)
- `available_to` (timestamp, nullable)
- `created_at` (timestamp)

### `reviews`
- `id` (uuid)
- `farmer_id` (uuid, FK)
- `approved` (boolean)
- `created_at` (timestamp)

### `media`
- `id` (uuid)
- `farmer_id` (uuid, FK)
- `type` (text: photo/video)
- `url` (text)
- `sort_order` (integer)

### `orders`
- `id` (uuid)
- `farmer_id` (uuid, FK)
- `created_at` (timestamp)

### `demand_intents`
- `crop_name` (text)
- `quantity_kg` (numeric, nullable)
- `needed_by_date` (date, nullable)
- `delivery_location` (text, nullable)
- `requester_name` (text)
- `requester_phone` (text)
- `region_slug` (text)
- `fulfilled` (boolean)

### `notify_requests`
- `farmer_id` (uuid, FK)
- `produce_name` (text)
- `requester_name` (text)
- `requester_phone` (text)

## Important Type Definitions

**Produce Listing in ProduceTab:**
```typescript
type Produce = {
  id: string
  name: string
  variety?: string
  emoji?: string
  method?: string
  status: string // 'available' | 'coming_soon'
  price_tier_1_qty?: number
  price_tier_1_price?: number
  price_tier_2_qty?: number
  price_tier_2_price?: number
  price_tier_3_qty?: number
  price_tier_3_price?: number
  stock_qty?: number
  brix?: number
  pesticide_result?: string
  shelf_life_days?: number
  available_to?: string
  description?: string
  image_url?: string
}
```

---

## API Endpoints

### `/api/produce` (GET)
**Query params:** `status?` (default: 'available')
**Returns:** Array of produce listings with farmer info
- Fetches all listings by status
- Joins with farmers (id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug, pickup_locations)
- Filters out listings from inactive farmers

### `/api/produce/search` (GET)
**Query params:** `q?`, `method?`, `category?`
**Returns:** Filtered produce array

---

## Authentication Flow

**Farmer login** (`/farmer/login`):
1. Enter 10-digit phone number
2. Query `farmers` table with multiple phone formats (digits, 0digits, +91digits, 91digits)
3. If not found, auto-create farmer with generated slug (`f-{digits}-{rand4}`)
4. Store farmer `id` and `slug` in localStorage
5. Redirect to `/farmer/dashboard`

---

## Localization

**Languages:** English (en), Telugu (te)
**Context:** `LanguageContext` (see `/src/lib/LanguageContext.tsx`)
**Storage:** `localStorage.yff_lang`

---

## Key Features Summary

| Feature | Location | Tech |
|---------|----------|------|
| **Farmer Profile (Public)** | `/farmer/[slug]` | Server-side rendering, async metadata |
| **Farmer Dashboard (Private)** | `/farmer/dashboard` | Client-side, localStorage auth |
| **Consumer Browse** | `/consumer` | Client-side, API fetch, search |
| **Shopping Cart** | `Cart.tsx` | localStorage, event dispatching |
| **WhatsApp Orders** | `Cart.tsx` | wa.me URL scheme |
| **Produce Management** | Dashboard modals | CRUD via Supabase |
| **Bilingual UI** | Context provider | React Context |

5	export type CartItem = {
6	  listingId: string
7	  qty: number
8	  name: string
9	  variety?: string
10	  emoji?: string
11	  pricePerKg?: number
12	  priceTier1Qty?: number
13	  priceTier1Price?: number

25	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
26	
27	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
28	
29	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
30	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
31	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
32	
33	const readCart = (): CartState => {
34	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
35	  try {
36	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
37	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
38	  } catch {
39	    return {}
40	  }
41	}
42	
43	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
44	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
45	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
46	}
47	
48	function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem): { price?: number; isDiscount: boolean } {
49	  const { priceTier1Qty, priceTier1Price, priceTier2Qty, priceTier2Price, priceTier3Price } = item
50	  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null)
51	    return { price: item.pricePerKg, isDiscount: false }
52	  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty)
53	    return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
54	  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {
55	    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return { price: priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
56	    return { price: priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
57	  }
58	  if (priceTier3Price != null) return { price: priceTier3Price, isDiscount: true }
59	  return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
60	}
61	
62	export function useCart() {
63	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
64	
65	  useEffect(() => {
66	    setCart(readCart())
67	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
68	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
69	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
70	    return () => {
71	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
72	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
73	    }
74	  }, [])
75	
76	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
77	    const next = { ...readCart() }
78	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
79	    const newQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)
80	    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }
81	    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)
82	    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }
83	    writeCart(next)
84	  }, [])
85	
86	  const setQty = useCallback((listingId: string, qty: number) => {
87	    const next = { ...readCart() }
88	    if (qty <= 0) delete next[listingId]
89	    else if (next[listingId]) {
90	      const updated = { ...next[listingId], qty }
91	      const { price } = getActiveTier(qty, updated)
92	      next[listingId] = { ...updated, pricePerKg: price ?? updated.pricePerKg }
93	    }
94	    writeCart(next)
95	  }, [])
96	
97	  const removeItem = useCallback((listingId: string) => {
98	    const next = { ...readCart() }
99	    delete next[listingId]
100	    writeCart(next)
101	  }, [])
102	
103	  const clear = useCallback(() => writeCart({}), [])
104	
105	  const items = Object.values(cart)
106	  const count = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.qty, 0)
107	
108	  return { cart, items, count, addItem, setQty, removeItem, clear }
109	}
110	
111	export function useConsumerInfo() {
112	  const [info, setInfo] = useState<ConsumerInfo>({ name: '', phone: '' })
113	
114	  useEffect(() => {
115	    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
116	    try {
117	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CONSUMER_KEY)
118	      if (raw) setInfo(JSON.parse(raw))
119	    } catch { /* ignore */ }
120	  }, [])
121	
122	  const save = useCallback((next: ConsumerInfo) => {
123	    setInfo(next)
124	    localStorage.setItem(CONSUMER_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
125	  }, [])
126	
127	  return { info, save }
128	}
129	
130	/* ─── Cart FAB + Sheet ───────────────────────────────── */
131	export function CartFab() {
132	  const { items, count } = useCart()
133	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
134	
135	  if (count === 0 && !open) return null
136	
137	  return (
138	    <>
139	      {!open && (
140	        <button
141	          onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
142	          className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-40 bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white rounded-full shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-2 pl-4 pr-5 py-3.5"
143	          aria-label="View cart"
144	        >
145	          <CartIcon />
146	          <span className="font-extrabold text-sm">
147	            {count} {count === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} / బుట్ట
148	          </span>
149	        </button>
150	      )}
151	      {open && (
152	        <CartSheet items={items} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
153	      )}
154	    </>
155	  )
156	}
157	
158	/* ─── Cart sheet ─────────────────────────────────────── */
159	function CartSheet({
160	  items,
161	  onClose,
162	}: {
163	  items: CartItem[]
164	  onClose: () => void
165	}) {
166	  const { setQty, removeItem, clear } = useCart()
167	  const { info, save: saveInfo } = useConsumerInfo()
168	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
169	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')

172	
173	  useEffect(() => {
174	    setName(info.name)
175	    setPhone(info.phone)
176	  }, [info])
177	
178	  // Group items by farmer
179	  const byFarmer = items.reduce<Record<string, CartItem[]>>((acc, item) => {
180	    (acc[item.farmerId] = acc[item.farmerId] ?? []).push(item)
181	    return acc
182	  }, {})
183	  const farmerGroups = Object.values(byFarmer)
184	
185	  const detailsMissing = !name.trim() || phone.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10
186	
187	  const handleOrderFarmer = (group: CartItem[]) => {
188	    if (detailsMissing) return
189	    saveInfo({ name: name.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })
190	
191	    const f = group[0]
192	    const lines = group.map((it) => {
193	      const price = it.pricePerKg ? ` @ ₹${it.pricePerKg}/kg` : ''
194	      return `• ${it.emoji ?? '🌿'} ${it.name}${it.variety ? ` (${it.variety})` : ''} — ${it.qty} kg${price}`
195	    })
196	
197	    const selectedPickup = pickupByFarmer[f.farmerId]
198	    const pickupLine = selectedPickup
199	      ? `\n\n*Pickup location / పికప్ స్థలం:* ${selectedPickup} (${f.farmerVillage})`
200	      : `\n\n*Pickup from your farm / మీ పొలం నుండి పికప్* (${f.farmerVillage})`
201	
202	    const msg =
203	      `Hello ${f.farmerName} anna! 🙏\n` +
204	      `I saw your produce on YourFamilyFarmer and would like to order:\n\n` +
205	      lines.join('\n') +
206	      pickupLine +
207	      `\n\nMy name / నా పేరు: ${name.trim()}\n` +

209	      `Please share a good pickup time. Thank you! / పికప్ సమయం తెలియజేయండి, ధన్యవాదాలు 🌱`
210	
211	    const digits = f.farmerPhone.replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '')
212	    const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits
213	    const waUrl = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`
214	
215	    window.open(waUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')
216	    setSentFarmers((s) => ({ ...s, [f.farmerId]: true }))
217	  }
218	
219	  return (
220	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
221	      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col">
222	        {/* Header */}

233	        {/* Body */}

235	          {items.length === 0 ? (

239	              <p className="text-sm mt-1">బుట్ట ఖాళీగా ఉంది</p>
240	            </div>
241	          ) : (
242	            <>
243	              {/* Consumer details */}

248	                  </label>

250	                    type="text"
251	                    value={name}
252	                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}

255	                  />

259	                    Your WhatsApp number / వాట్సాప్ నంబర్
260	                  </label>
261	                  <div className="flex gap-2">

282	              {/* Farmer groups */}
283	              {farmerGroups.map((group) => {

307	                    <div className="p-3 space-y-2">

338	                        </div>

340	
341	                      {total > 0 && (

344	                          <span className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">
345	                            ₹{total}
346	                          </span>
347	                        </div>
348	                      )}
349	
350	                      {f.farmerPickupLocations && f.farmerPickupLocations.length > 0 && (
351	                        <div className="pt-2">
352	                          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1">
353	                            Pickup location / పికప్ స్థలం
354	                          </label>
355	                          <select

364	                          >

394	                  </div>

396	              })}
397	
398	              {farmerGroups.length > 1 && (

400	                  One WhatsApp chat opens per farmer, since each farm handles its own pickup.<br />

408	              >

410	              </button>
411	            </>

419	function QtyStepper({
420	  qty,
421	  onDec,
422	  onInc,
423	}: {
424	  qty: number
425	  onDec: () => void
426	  onInc: () => void
427	}) {
428	  return (
429	    <div className="flex items-center border border-gray-200 rounded-full">
430	      <button
431	        onClick={onDec}
432	        className="w-8 h-8 text-lg text-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-100 rounded-l-full"
433	        aria-label="Decrease"
434	      >
435	        −
436	      </button>
437	      <span className="w-10 text-center font-bold text-sm text-gray-900">
438	        {qty}
439	      </span>
440	      <button
441	        onClick={onInc}
442	        className="w-8 h-8 text-lg text-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-100 rounded-r-full"
443	        aria-label="Increase"
444	      >
445	        +
446	      </button>
447	    </div>
448	  )
449	}

3	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'

7	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
8	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
9	
10	type Farmer = {

12	  name: string
13	  slug: string
14	  village: string
15	  district: string
16	  phone: string
17	  method: string
18	  region_slug: string

27	  total_qty: number
28	}
29	
30	type ListingRow = {
31	  id: string
32	  name: string
33	  variety: string | null
34	  emoji: string | null
35	  status: string

41	}

45	  variety: string
46	  emoji: string
47	  price: string
48	  method: string
49	  stock: string
50	}
51	
52	const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾', '🥥']
53	
54	const isProfileComplete = (f: Farmer | null) =>
55	  !!f && f.name?.trim().length > 0 && f.village?.trim().length > 0
56	
57	export default function FarmerDashboard() {
58	  const router = useRouter()
59	  const { tx } = useLang()
60	  const [farmer, setFarmer] = useState<Farmer | null>(null)
61	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
62	  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false)
63	  const [activeListings, setActiveListings] = useState(0)
64	  const [ordersCount, setOrdersCount] = useState(0)
65	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
66	  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
67	  const [showProfileEdit, setShowProfileEdit] = useState(false)
68	  const [showListings, setShowListings] = useState(false)
69	
70	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
71	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
72	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
73	
74	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
75	      .from('farmers')
76	      .select('*')
77	      .eq('id', farmerId)
78	      .maybeSingle()
79	
80	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
81	    setFarmer(farmerData)
82	
83	    const [listingsRes, ordersRes, intentsRes] = await Promise.all([
84	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),
85	      supabase.from('orders').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
86	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
87	    ])
88	
89	    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)
90	    setOrdersCount(ordersRes.count ?? 0)
91	
92	    const map: Record<string, number> = {}
93	    for (const row of intentsRes.data ?? []) {
94	      map[row.crop_name] = (map[row.crop_name] ?? 0) + (Number(row.quantity_kg) || 0)
95	    }
96	    setDemandBars(
97	      Object.entries(map)
98	        .map(([crop_name, total_qty]) => ({ crop_name, total_qty }))
99	        .sort((a, b) => b.total_qty - a.total_qty)
100	        .slice(0, 5)
101	    )
102	
103	    setLoading(false)
104	  }, [router])
105	
106	  useEffect(() => { loadDashboard() }, [loadDashboard])
107	
108	  // Auto-open the profile edit modal the first time an incomplete farmer lands here.
109	  useEffect(() => {
110	    if (!loading && farmer && !isProfileComplete(farmer)) {
111	      setShowProfileEdit(true)
112	    }
113	  }, [loading, farmer])
114	
115	  const handleLogout = () => {
116	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_id')
117	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_slug')
118	    router.replace('/farmer/login')
119	  }
120	
121	  if (loading) return <LoadingScreen />
122	  if (notFound) return <FarmerNotFound onLogout={handleLogout} />
123	
124	  const profileComplete = isProfileComplete(farmer)
125	  const displayName = farmer!.name?.trim() || tx.welcome
126	
127	  return (
128	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
129	      {/* Header */}
130	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
131	        <div className="flex justify-end mb-2">
132	          <LanguageToggle />
133	        </div>
134	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
135	          <div>
136	            <p className="text-green-400 text-xs font-semibold mb-0.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
137	              {tx.farmerDashboard}
138	            </p>
139	            <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{displayName}</h1>
140	            <p className="text-green-300 text-sm mt-0.5">
141	              {profileComplete
142	                ? `${farmer!.village}, ${farmer!.district}`
143	                : tx.completeProfilePrompt}
144	            </p>
145	            <p className="text-green-500 text-xs mt-1">+91 {farmer!.phone}</p>
146	          </div>
147	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
148	            {profileComplete ? (
149	              <Link
150	                href={`/farmer/${farmer!.slug}`}
151	                className="bg-white text-green-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl"
152	              >
153	                {tx.viewProfile} ↗
154	              </Link>
155	            ) : (
156	              <span className="bg-amber-400 text-amber-900 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
157	                {tx.incomplete}
158	              </span>
159	            )}
160	            <button
161	              onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
162	              className="text-white text-xs underline"
163	            >
164	              {tx.editProfile}
165	            </button>
166	            <button onClick={handleLogout} className="text-green-500 text-xs underline">
167	              {tx.logout}
168	            </button>
169	          </div>
170	        </div>
171	      </div>
172	
173	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
174	        {/* Complete profile banner */}
175	        {!profileComplete && (
176	          <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
177	            <span className="text-2xl flex-shrink-0">📝</span>
178	            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
179	              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
180	                {tx.completeProfileTitle}
181	              </h3>
182	              <p className="text-amber-700 text-xs mt-0.5">
183	                {tx.completeProfileHelp}
184	              </p>
185	              <button
186	                onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
187	                className="mt-3 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm"
188	              >
189	                {tx.fillDetails}
190	              </button>
191	            </div>
192	          </div>
193	        )}
194	
195	        {/* Stat cards */}
196	        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
197	          <button
198	            onClick={() => setShowListings(true)}
199	            className="border-green-200 bg-green-50 border rounded-2xl p-4 text-left active:bg-green-100 relative"
200	          >
201	            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800">{activeListings}</div>
202	            <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1 leading-tight">{tx.activeListings}</div>
203	            <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-700 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1">
204	              {tx.manage} <span aria-hidden>→</span>
205	            </div>
206	          </button>
207	          {[
208	            { label: tx.ordersThisWeek, value: ordersCount, color: 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50', vcolor: 'text-blue-800' },
209	            { label: tx.avgRating, value: farmer!.rating_avg ? `${Number(farmer!.rating_avg).toFixed(1)} ★` : '—', color: 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50', vcolor: 'text-amber-800' },
210	            { label: tx.totalBuyers, value: farmer!.buyer_count ?? 0, color: 'border-purple-200 bg-purple-50', vcolor: 'text-purple-800' },
211	          ].map((s) => (
212	            <div key={s.label} className={`${s.color} border rounded-2xl p-4`}>
213	              <div className={`text-3xl font-black ${s.vcolor}`}>{s.value}</div>
214	              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1 leading-tight">{s.label}</div>
215	            </div>
216	          ))}
217	        </div>
218	
219	        {/* Demand chart */}
220	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
221	          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
222	            {tx.localDemand}
223	          </h2>
224	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mb-4">
225	            {tx.localDemandHelp}
226	          </p>
227	
228	          {demandBars.length === 0 ? (
229	            <div className="text-center py-6">
230	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noDemandSignals}</p>
231	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-1">{tx.shareProfileLink}</p>
232	            </div>
233	          ) : (
234	            <div className="space-y-3">
235	              {demandBars.map((bar) => {
236	                const pct = Math.round((bar.total_qty / demandBars[0].total_qty) * 100)
237	                return (
238	                  <div key={bar.crop_name}>
239	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
240	                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">{bar.crop_name}</span>
241	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">{bar.total_qty} kg</span>
242	                    </div>
243	                    <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
244	                      <div
245	                        className="h-full bg-green-600 rounded-full"
246	                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
247	                      />
248	                    </div>
249	                  </div>
250	                )
251	              })}
252	            </div>
253	          )}
254	        </div>
255	
256	        {/* Add listing button */}
257	        {profileComplete ? (
258	          !showForm && (
259	            <button
260	              onClick={() => setShowForm(true)}
261	              className="w-full bg-white border-2 border-green-700 text-green-700 font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:bg-green-50"
262	            >
263	              <span className="text-xl leading-none">+</span>
264	              {tx.addNewProduce}
265	            </button>
266	          )
267	        ) : (
268	          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 font-semibold py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm text-center">
269	            {tx.completeBeforeAdd}
270	          </div>
271	        )}
272	
273	        {/* Listing form */}
274	        {showForm && profileComplete && (
275	          <ProduceListingForm
276	            farmerId={farmer!.id}
277	            farmerSlug={farmer!.slug}
278	            defaultMethod={farmer!.method}
279	            onClose={() => setShowForm(false)}
280	            onPublished={() => { setShowForm(false); loadDashboard() }}
281	          />
282	        )}
283	      </div>
284	
285	      {/* Manage listings modal */}
286	      {showListings && farmer && (
287	        <ManageListingsModal
288	          farmerId={farmer.id}
289	          onClose={() => setShowListings(false)}
290	          onChanged={loadDashboard}
291	        />
292	      )}
293	
294	      {/* Edit profile modal */}
295	      {showProfileEdit && farmer && (
296	        <ProfileEditModal
297	          farmer={farmer}
298	          onClose={() => {
299	            // If profile still incomplete, don't allow close (keep banner as fallback)
300	            if (isProfileComplete(farmer)) setShowProfileEdit(false)
301	            else setShowProfileEdit(false) // allow dismiss; banner still shown
302	          }}
303	          onSaved={(updated) => {
304	            setFarmer(updated)
305	            setShowProfileEdit(false)
306	          }}
307	        />
308	      )}
309	    </main>
310	  )
311	}
312	
313	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */
314	function ProfileEditModal({
315	  farmer,
316	  onClose,
317	  onSaved,
318	}: {
319	  farmer: Farmer
320	  onClose: () => void
321	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
322	}) {
323	  const { tx } = useLang()
324	  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')
325	  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')
326	  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')
327	  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')
328	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
329	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
330	  )
331	  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(
332	    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],
333	  )
334	  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')
335	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
336	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
337	
338	  const addPickup = () => {
339	    const v = newPickup.trim()
340	    if (!v) return
341	    if (pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }
342	    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v])
343	    setNewPickup('')
344	  }
345	  const removePickup = (loc: string) =>
346	    setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))
347	
348	  const handleSave = async () => {
349	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.nameRequired); return }
350	    if (!village.trim()) { setError(tx.villageRequired); return }
351	    setLoading(true)
352	    setError('')
353	
354	    // Build a unique slug from the name once the farmer actually has one.
355	    // Only regenerate slug if current slug still looks auto-generated (f-<digits>-xxxx).
356	    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')
357	    const baseSlug = name
358	      .toLowerCase()
359	      .trim()
360	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
361	      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
362	      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'
363	
364	    let newSlug = farmer.slug
365	    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {
366	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)
367	      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`
368	    }
369	
370	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
371	      name: name.trim(),
372	      village: village.trim(),
373	      district: district.trim(),
374	      method,
375	      slug: newSlug,
376	      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,
377	    }
378	    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)
379	
380	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
381	      .from('farmers')
382	      .update(payload)
383	      .eq('id', farmer.id)
384	      .select('*')
385	      .single()
386	
387	    setLoading(false)
388	
389	    if (err || !data) {
390	      setError(err?.message ?? tx.couldNotSave)
391	      return
392	    }
393	
394	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)
395	    onSaved(data)
396	  }
397	
398	  return (
399	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
400	      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
401	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
402	          <div>
403	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">
404	              {tx.profileModalTitle}
405	            </h3>
406	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{tx.profileModalSubtitle}</p>
407	          </div>
408	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
409	        </div>
410	
411	        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
412	          <Field
413	            label={tx.yourNameLabel}
414	            placeholder="Ramu Reddy"
415	            value={name}
416	            onChange={setName}
417	          />
418	          <Field
419	            label={tx.villageLabel}
420	            placeholder="Tadepalligudem"
421	            value={village}
422	            onChange={setVillage}
423	          />
424	          <Field
425	            label={tx.districtLabel}
426	            placeholder="West Godavari"
427	            value={district}
428	            onChange={setDistrict}
429	          />
430	
431	          <div>
432	            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
433	              {tx.farmingMethodLabel}
434	            </label>
435	            <select
436	              value={method}
437	              onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
438	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
439	            >
440	              <option value="natural">{tx.methodNatural}</option>
441	              <option value="organic">{tx.methodOrganic}</option>
442	              <option value="low_chemical">{tx.methodLowChemical}</option>
443	              <option value="chemical">{tx.methodChemical}</option>
444	            </select>
445	          </div>
446	
447	          <Field
448	            label={tx.farmingSinceLabel}
449	            placeholder="e.g. 2005"
450	            value={sinceYear}
451	            onChange={setSinceYear}
452	            type="number"
453	          />
454	
455	          <div>
456	            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
457	              {tx.pickupLocationsLabel}
458	            </label>
459	            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2 leading-snug">
460	              {tx.pickupLocationsHelp}
461	            </p>
462	            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
463	              <input
464	                type="text"
465	                placeholder={tx.pickupPlaceholder}
466	                value={newPickup}
467	                onChange={(e) => setNewPickup(e.target.value)}
468	                onKeyDown={(e) => {
469	                  if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); addPickup() }
470	                }}
471	                className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
472	              />
473	              <button
474	                type="button"
475	                onClick={addPickup}
476	                className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-4 rounded-xl text-sm"
477	              >
478	                {tx.addBtn}
479	              </button>
480	            </div>
481	            {pickupLocations.length > 0 && (
482	              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
483	                {pickupLocations.map((loc) => (
484	                  <span
485	                    key={loc}
486	                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 text-green-800 rounded-full pl-3 pr-1 py-1 text-xs font-semibold"
487	                  >
488	                    📍 {loc}
489	                    <button
490	                      type="button"
491	                      onClick={() => removePickup(loc)}
492	                      className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-green-200 text-green-800 text-sm leading-none flex items-center justify-center"
493	                      aria-label={`Remove ${loc}`}
494	                    >
495	                      ×
496	                    </button>
497	                  </span>
498	                ))}
499	              </div>
500	            )}
501	          </div>
502	
503	          {error && (
504	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
505	          )}
506	
507	          <button
508	            onClick={handleSave}
509	            disabled={loading || !name.trim() || !village.trim()}
510	            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800"
511	          >
512	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.saveProfile}
513	          </button>
514	        </div>
515	      </div>
516	    </div>
517	  )
518	}
519	
520	function Field({
521	  label,
522	  placeholder,
523	  value,
524	  onChange,
525	  type = 'text',
526	}: {
527	  label: string
528	  placeholder?: string
529	  value: string
530	  onChange: (v: string) => void
531	  type?: string
532	}) {
533	  return (
534	    <div>
535	      <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
536	        {label}
537	      </label>
538	      <input
539	        type={type}
540	        placeholder={placeholder}
541	        value={value}
542	        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
543	        className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
544	      />
545	    </div>
546	  )
547	}
548	
549	/* ─── Produce listing form ──────────────────────────────────── */
550	function ProduceListingForm({
551	  farmerId,
552	  farmerSlug,
553	  defaultMethod,
554	  onClose,
555	  onPublished,
556	}: {
557	  farmerId: string
558	  farmerSlug: string
559	  defaultMethod: string
560	  onClose: () => void
561	  onPublished: () => void
562	}) {
563	  const { tx } = useLang()
564	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
565	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
566	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')
567	  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
568	  const [period, setPeriod] = useState('')
569	  const [farmingMethod, setFarmingMethod] = useState(defaultMethod || 'natural')
570	  const [price1, setPrice1] = useState('')
571	  const [price1Qty, setPrice1Qty] = useState('5')
572	  const [price2, setPrice2] = useState('')
573	  const [price2Qty, setPrice2Qty] = useState('20')
574	  const [price3, setPrice3] = useState('')
575	  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
576	  const [brix, setBrix] = useState('')
577	  const [soc, setSoc] = useState('')
578	  const [imageFile, setImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
579	  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string>('')
580	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
581	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
582	  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(false)
583	  const [published, setPublished] = useState(false)
584	  const [publishedSlug, setPublishedSlug] = useState('')
585	
586	  const handlePickImage = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
587	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
588	    if (!file) return
589	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
590	      setError(tx.pickImageFile)
591	      return
592	    }
593	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) {
594	      setError(tx.imageTooLarge)
595	      return
596	    }
597	    setError('')
598	    if (imagePreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview)
599	    setImageFile(file)
600	    setImagePreview(URL.createObjectURL(file))
601	  }
602	
603	  const clearImage = () => {
604	    if (imagePreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview)
605	    setImageFile(null)
606	    setImagePreview('')
607	  }
608	
609	  const uploadImage = async (file: File): Promise<string | null> => {
610	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
611	    const path = `${farmerId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}.${ext}`
612	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
613	      .from('farm-images')
614	      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
615	    if (upErr) {
616	      setError(`Image upload failed: ${upErr.message}`)
617	      return null
618	    }
619	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
620	    return data.publicUrl
621	  }
622	
623	  const previewData: PreviewData = {
624	    name: name || 'Produce name',
625	    variety: variety || '',
626	    emoji,
627	    price: price1 || '—',
628	    method: farmingMethod,
629	    stock: qty || '—',
630	  }
631	
632	  const handlePublish = async () => {
633	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.produceNameRequired); return }
634	    setLoading(true)
635	    setError('')
636	
637	    let imageUrl: string | null = null
638	    if (imageFile) {
639	      imageUrl = await uploadImage(imageFile)
640	      if (!imageUrl) { setLoading(false); return }
641	    }
642	
643	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
644	      farmer_id: farmerId,
645	      name: name.trim(),
646	      emoji,
647	      status: 'available',
648	      method: farmingMethod,
649	    }
650	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
651	    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)
652	    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()
653	    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)
654	    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)
655	    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }
656	    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }
657	    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }
658	    if (imageUrl) payload.image_url = imageUrl
659	
660	    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload)
661	    setLoading(false)
662	
663	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
664	
665	    setPublished(true)
666	    setPublishedSlug(farmerSlug)
667	    setTimeout(() => onPublished(), 2500)
668	  }
669	
670	  if (published) {
671	    return (
672	      <div className="bg-green-50 border-2 border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center space-y-3">
673	        <div className="text-4xl">✅</div>
674	        <p className="font-extrabold text-green-800 text-lg">{tx.publishedTitle}</p>
675	        <p className="text-green-700 text-sm">{tx.listingLive}</p>
676	        <Link
677	          href={`/farmer/${publishedSlug}`}
678	          className="inline-block bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
679	        >
680	          {tx.viewYourProfile} ↗
681	        </Link>
682	      </div>
683	    )
684	  }
685	
686	  return (
687	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
688	      {/* Form header */}
689	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
690	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">
691	          {tx.newProduceListing}
692	        </h3>
693	        <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
694	      </div>
695	
696	      <div className="p-4 space-y-5">
697	        {/* Emoji picker */}
698	        <div>
699	          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-2">{tx.pickIcon}</p>
700	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
701	            {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
702	              <button
703	                key={e}
704	                onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
705	                className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
706	                  emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'
707	                }`}
708	              >
709	                {e}
710	              </button>
711	            ))}
712	          </div>
713	        </div>
714	
715	        {/* Name + variety */}
716	        <div className="space-y-2">
717	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
718	            {tx.produceDetails}
719	          </label>
720	          <input
721	            type="text"
722	            placeholder={tx.produceNamePlaceholder}
723	            value={name}
724	            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
725	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
726	          />
727	          <input
728	            type="text"
729	            placeholder={tx.varietyPlaceholder}
730	            value={variety}
731	            onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
732	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
733	          />
734	        </div>
735	
736	        {/* Qty + period */}
737	        <div className="space-y-2">
738	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
739	            {tx.availability}
740	          </label>
741	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
742	            <input
743	              type="number"
744	              placeholder={tx.quantityPlaceholder}
745	              value={qty}
746	              onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
747	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
748	            />
749	            <input
750	              type="text"
751	              placeholder={tx.periodPlaceholder}
752	              value={period}
753	              onChange={(e) => setPeriod(e.target.value)}
754	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
755	            />
756	          </div>
757	        </div>
758	
759	        {/* Farming method */}
760	        <div className="space-y-2">
761	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
762	            {tx.farmingMethodLabel}
763	          </label>
764	          <select
765	            value={farmingMethod}
766	            onChange={(e) => setFarmingMethod(e.target.value)}

992	/* ─── Preview modal ─────────────────────────────────────────── */
993	function PreviewModal({ data, onClose }: { data: PreviewData; onClose: () => void }) {
994	  const { tx } = useLang()
995	  const EMOJI_BG: Record<string, string> = {
996	    '🍅': 'bg-red-100', '🥬': 'bg-green-100', '🥭': 'bg-orange-100',
997	    '🍆': 'bg-purple-100', '🥕': 'bg-orange-100', '🌽': 'bg-yellow-100',
998	    '🍌': 'bg-yellow-100', '🫑': 'bg-green-100', '🌿': 'bg-green-50',
999	    '🌾': 'bg-amber-100', '🍓': 'bg-red-50',
1000	  }
1001	  const bg = EMOJI_BG[data.emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
1002	
1003	  return (
1004	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end justify-center p-4">
1005	      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden">
1006	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
1007	          <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">
1008	            {tx.previewHeading}
1009	          </p>
1010	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none">×</button>
1011	        </div>
1012	        <div className="p-4">
1013	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden max-w-[180px] mx-auto">
1014	            <div className={`${bg} flex items-center justify-center py-8`}>
1015	              <span className="text-5xl">{data.emoji}</span>
1016	            </div>
1017	            <div className="p-3">
1018	              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">{data.name}</h3>
1019	              {data.variety && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{data.variety}</p>}
1020	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">
1021	                <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
1022	                  {data.price !== '—' ? `₹${data.price}` : '—'}
1023	                  <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/kg</span>
1024	                </span>
1025	                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800">
1026	                  {data.method}
1027	                </span>
1028	              </div>
1029	              {data.stock !== '—' && (
1030	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{data.stock} kg left</p>
1031	              )}
1032	              <div className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-center">
1033	                {tx.previewOrderBtn}
1034	              </div>
1035	            </div>
1036	          </div>
1037	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center mt-3">
1038	            {tx.previewFooter}
1039	          </p>
1040	        </div>
1041	        <div className="px-4 pb-4">
1042	          <button
1043	            onClick={onClose}
1044	            className="w-full border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
1045	          >
1046	            {tx.close}
1047	          </button>
1048	        </div>
1049	      </div>
1050	    </div>
1051	  )
1052	}

1054	/* ─── Manage listings modal ────────────────────────────────── */
1055	function ManageListingsModal({
1056	  farmerId,
1057	  onClose,
1058	  onChanged,
1059	}: {
1060	  farmerId: string
1061	  onClose: () => void
1062	  onChanged: () => void
1063	}) {
1064	  const { tx } = useLang()
1065	  const [rows, setRows] = useState<ListingRow[]>([])
1066	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
1067	  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null)
1068	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
1069	
1070	  const load = useCallback(async () => {
1071	    setLoading(true)
1072	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
1073	      .from('produce_listings')
1074	      .select('id, name, variety, emoji, status, method, stock_qty, price_tier_1_price, image_url, created_at')
1075	      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
1076	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
1077	    setLoading(false)
1078	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
1079	    setRows((data ?? []) as ListingRow[])
1080	  }, [farmerId])
1081	
1082	  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
1083	
1084	  const handleDelete = async (row: ListingRow) => {
1085	    if (!confirm(tx.confirmDelete.replace('{name}', row.name))) return
1086	
1087	    setDeletingId(row.id)
1088	    setError('')
1089	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
1090	      .from('produce_listings')
1091	      .delete()
1092	      .eq('id', row.id)
1093	      .select('id')
1094	    setDeletingId(null)
1095	
1096	    if (err) {
1097	      setError(err.message)
1098	      return
1099	    }
1100	    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
1101	      setError(tx.deleteBlocked)
1102	      return
1103	    }
1104	    setRows((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== row.id))
1105	    onChanged()
1106	  }
1107	
1108	  return (
1109	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
1110	      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
1111	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
1112	          <div>
1113	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
1114	              {tx.yourProduce}
1115	            </h3>
1116	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{tx.manageOrDelete}</p>
1117	          </div>
1118	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
1119	        </div>
1120	
1121	        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
1122	          {loading && (
1123	            <div className="text-center py-10">
1124	              <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
1125	              <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-3">{tx.loadingLabel}</p>
1126	            </div>
1127	          )}
1128	
1129	          {!loading && error && (
1130	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
1131	          )}
1132	
1133	          {!loading && rows.length === 0 && !error && (
1134	            <div className="text-center py-10">
1135	              <div className="text-5xl mb-2">🌾</div>
1136	              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm">{tx.noProduceYet}</p>
1137	            </div>
1138	          )}
1139	
1140	          {!loading && rows.map((row) => (
1141	            <ListingRowCard
1142	              key={row.id}
1143	              row={row}
1144	              deleting={deletingId === row.id}
1145	              onDelete={() => handleDelete(row)}
1146	            />
1147	          ))}
1148	        </div>
1149	
1150	        <div className="sticky bottom-0 bg-white border-t border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">
1151	          <button
1152	            onClick={onClose}
1153	            className="w-full border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
1154	          >
1155	            {tx.close}
1156	          </button>
1157	        </div>
1158	      </div>
1159	    </div>
1160	  )
1161	}
1162	
1163	function ListingRowCard({
1164	  row,
1165	  deleting,
1166	  onDelete,
1167	}: {
1168	  row: ListingRow
1169	  deleting: boolean
1170	  onDelete: () => void
1171	}) {
1172	  const { tx } = useLang()
1173	  const emoji = row.emoji ?? '🌿'
1174	  const statusLabel =
1175	    row.status === 'available'
1176	      ? tx.availableLabel
1177	      : row.status === 'coming_soon'
1178	      ? tx.comingSoon
1179	      : row.status
1180	  const statusColor =
1181	    row.status === 'available'
1182	      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
1183	      : row.status === 'coming_soon'
1184	      ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
1185	      : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
1186	
1187	  return (
1188	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
1189	      <div className="flex gap-3 p-3">
1190	        {row.image_url ? (
1191	          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
1192	          <img
1193	            src={row.image_url}
1194	            alt={row.name}
1195	            loading="lazy"
1196	            className="rounded-xl w-16 h-16 object-cover flex-shrink-0 bg-gray-100"
1197	          />
1198	        ) : (
1199	          <div className="bg-green-50 rounded-xl w-16 h-16 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl flex-shrink-0">
1200	            {emoji}
1201	          </div>
1202	        )}
1203	        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
1204	          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
1205	            <div className="min-w-0">
1206	              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm truncate">{row.name}</h4>
1207	              {row.variety && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{row.variety}</p>}
1208	            </div>
1209	            <span className={`${statusColor} text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap`}>
1210	              {statusLabel}
1211	            </span>
1212	          </div>
1213	          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
1214	            {row.price_tier_1_price != null && (
1215	              <span className="font-semibold text-green-700">
1216	                ₹{row.price_tier_1_price}
1217	                <span className="text-gray-400 font-normal">/kg</span>
1218	              </span>
1219	            )}
1220	            {row.stock_qty != null && (
1221	              <span>{row.stock_qty} {tx.kgLabel}</span>
1222	            )}
1223	            {row.method && (
1224	              <span className="bg-green-50 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
1225	                {row.method}
1226	              </span>
1227	            )}
1228	          </div>
1229	        </div>
1230	      </div>
1231	
1232	      <div className="px-3 pb-3">
1233	        <button
1234	          onClick={onDelete}
1235	          disabled={deleting}
1236	          className="w-full border border-red-200 text-red-600 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
1237	        >
1238	          {deleting ? tx.deleting : `🗑 ${tx.deleteProduce}`}
1239	        </button>
1240	      </div>
1241	    </div>
1242	  )
1243	}
1244	
1245	/* ─── Loading screen ────────────────────────────────────────── */
1246	function LoadingScreen() {
1247	  const { tx } = useLang()
1248	  return (
1249	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center">
1250	      <div className="text-center space-y-3">
1251	        <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
1252	        <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.loadingLabel}</p>
1253	      </div>
1254	    </main>
1255	  )
1256	}
1257	
1258	/* ─── Farmer not found ──────────────────────────────────────── */
1259	function FarmerNotFound({ onLogout }: { onLogout: () => void }) {
1260	  const { tx } = useLang()
1261	  return (

1271	    </main>
1272	  )

508	
509	export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof t.en
510	
511	export function bilingual(key: TranslationKey): string {
512	  const en = (t.en as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
513	  const te = (t.te as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
514	  if (!te || en === te) return en
515	  // Long / multi-line text (e.g. WhatsApp templates, descriptions) → stack with separator.
516	  if (en.length > 60 || en.includes('\n') || te.includes('\n')) {
517	    return `${en}\n\n---\n\n${te}`
518	  }
519	  return `${en} / ${te}`
520	}
521	
```

  "new_string": "  const handleOrderFarmer = (group: CartItem[]) => {\n    if (detailsMissing) return\n    saveInfo({ name: name.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })\n\n    const f = group[0]\n    const lines = group.map((it) => {\n      const unit = it.unit || 'kg'\n      const price = it.pricePerKg ? ` @ ₹${it.pricePerKg}/${unit}` : ''\n      return `• ${it.emoji ?? '🌿'} ${it.name}${it.variety ? ` (${it.variety})` : ''} — ${it.qty} ${unit}${price}`\n    })\n\n    const selectedPickup = pickupByFarmer[f.farmerId]\n    const pickupLine = selectedPickup\n      ? `\\n\\n*Pickup location / పికప్ స్థలం:* ${selectedPickup} (${f.farmerVillage})`\n      : `\\n\\n*Pickup from your farm / మీ పొలం నుండి పికప్* (${f.farmerVillage})`\n\n    const msg =\n      `Hello ${f.farmerName} anna! 🙏\\n` +\n      `I saw your produce on YourFamilyFarmer and would like to order:\\n\\n` +\n      lines.join('\\n') +\n      pickupLine +\n      `\\n\\nMy name / నా పేరు: ${name.trim()}\\n` +\n      `My WhatsApp / నా వాట్సాప్: +91 ${phone.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)}\\n\\n` +\n      `Please share a good pickup time.   "old_string": "  const handlePublish = async () => {\n    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.produceNameRequired); return }\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n\n    let imageUrl: string | null = null\n    if (imageFile) {\n      imageUrl = await uploadImage(imageFile)\n      if (!imageUrl) { setLoading(false); return }\n    }\n\n    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      farmer_id: farmerId,\n      name: name.trim(),\n      emoji,\n      status: 'available',\n      method: farmingMethod,\n    }\n    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()\n    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)\n    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()\n    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)\n    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)\n    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }\n    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }\n    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }\n    if (imageUrl) payload.image_url = imageUrl\n\n    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload)\n    setLoading(false)\n\n    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }\n\n    setPublished(true)\n    setPublishedSlug(farmerSlug)\n    setTimeout(() => onPublished(), 2500)\n  }",
  "new_string": "  const handlePublish = async () => {\n    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.produceNameRequired); return }\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n\n    let imageUrl: string | null = null\n    if (imageFile) {\n      imageUrl = await uploadImage(imageFile)\n      if (!imageUrl) { setLoading(false); return }\n    } else if (existingImageUrl) {\n      imageUrl = existingImageUrl\n    }\n\n    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      name: name.trim(),\n      emoji,\n      method: farmingMethod,\n      unit,\n    }\n    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()\n    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)\n    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()\n    else payload.description = null\n    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)\n    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)\n    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }\n    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }\n    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }\n    payload.image_url = imageUrl\n\n    if (isEdit && editData) {\n      const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').update(payload).eq('id', editData.id)\n      setLoading(false)\n      if (err) { setError(err.message); return }\n      onPublished()\n      return\n    }\n\n    const insertPayload = { ...payload, farmer_id: farmerId, status: 'available' }\n    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(insertPayload)\n    setLoading(false)\n\n    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }\n\n    setPublished(true)\n    setPublishedSlug(farmerSlug)\n    setTimeout(() => onPublished(), 2500)\n  }"
}

> AGENT

Build passes cleanly with no TypeScript errors. Here's what you need to do manually in Supabase SQL Editor:

---

## Manual SQL — run all of this in Supabase SQL Editor

```sql
-- Issue 1: Add missing columns
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS soil_organic_carbon numeric(4,2);
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS description text;

-- Issue 6: Unit field
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS unit text DEFAULT 'kg';

-- Issue 2: WhatsApp click tracking table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wa_clicks (
  id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  farmer_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  clicked_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE wa_clicks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "anyone can insert wa_clicks" ON wa_clicks FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "anyone can read wa_clicks" ON wa_clicks FOR SELECT USING (true);
```

---

## What was done in code

| # | Fix | Where |
|---|-----|-------|
| 1 | SQL columns — see above | Supabase (manual) |
| 2 | `wa_clicks` table counts button taps; dashboard "Orders this week" → "WhatsApp clicks" showing last-7-day count | `dashboard/page.tsx`, `Cart.tsx` |
| 3 | After buyer taps Send to farmer, that farmer's items are cleared from cart immediately | `Cart.tsx` — `clearFarmer()` called in `handleOrderFarmer` |
| 4 | Stock qty and price now show bold with unit label in Manage listings | `ListingRowCard` in `dashboard/page.tsx` |
| 5 | "Edit / సవరించు" button on each listing opens the same form pre-filled; saves via `UPDATE` | `ManageListingsModal` + `ProduceListingForm` edit mode |
| 6 | Unit dropdown (kg / gram / piece/నగ / bunch/కట్ట / litre) in add/edit form; unit appears in pricing tiers, cart price, and WhatsApp message | `dashboard/page.tsx` + `Cart.tsx` + `CartItem.unit` |
