41	  { key: 'fruits',     en: 'Fruits',          te: 'పళ్ళు' },
42	  { key: 'grains',     en: 'Grains & Pulses', te: 'ధాన్యాలు' },
43	  { key: 'leafy',      en: 'Leafy Greens',    te: 'ఆకు కూరలు' },
44	]
45	
46	const EMOJI_BG: Record<string, string> = {
47	  '🍅': 'bg-red-100',    '🥬': 'bg-green-100',  '🥭': 'bg-orange-100',
48	  '🍆': 'bg-purple-100', '🥕': 'bg-orange-100', '🌽': 'bg-yellow-100',
49	  '🍌': 'bg-yellow-100', '🫑': 'bg-green-100',  '🧅': 'bg-orange-50',
50	  '🧄': 'bg-amber-50',   '🍓': 'bg-red-50',     '🥦': 'bg-green-100',
51	  '🌾': 'bg-amber-100',  '🫒': 'bg-green-100',  '🌿': 'bg-green-50',
52	  '🥥': 'bg-orange-50',  '🍇': 'bg-purple-50',  '🍋': 'bg-yellow-50',
53	}
54	
55	const METHOD_STYLE: Record<string, string> = {
56	  natural:      'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
57	  organic:      'bg-teal-100  text-teal-800',
58	  low_chemical: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
59	  chemical:     'bg-red-100   text-red-700',
60	}
61	
62	const METHOD_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
63	  natural:      'Natural / సహజం',
64	  organic:      'Organic / సేంద్రీయ',
65	  low_chemical: 'Low chemical',
66	  chemical:     'Chemical',
67	}
68	
69	export default function ConsumerPage() {
70	  const [available, setAvailable]     = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
71	  const [comingSoon, setComingSoon]   = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
72	  const [filtered, setFiltered]       = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
73	  const [search, setSearch]           = useState('')
74	  const [method, setMethod]           = useState('all')
75	  const [category, setCategory]       = useState('all')
76	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(true)
77	  const [farmerCount, setFarmerCount] = useState(0)
78	
79	  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [])
80	
81	  const fetchData = async () => {
82	    setLoading(true)
83	    try {
84	      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([
85	        fetch('/api/produce'),
86	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon'),
87	      ])
88	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
89	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
90	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
91	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []
92	      setAvailable(avArr)
93	      setFiltered(avArr)
94	      setComingSoon(csArr)
95	      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)
96	    } catch { /* silent */ }
97	    setLoading(false)
98	  }
99	
100	  const doSearch = useCallback(async () => {
101	    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
102	      setFiltered(available)
103	      return
104	    }
105	    try {
106	      const p = new URLSearchParams()
107	      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
108	      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
109	      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
110	      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`)
111	      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
112	      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
113	    } catch { /* silent */ }
114	  }, [search, method, category, available])
115	
116	  useEffect(() => {
117	    const t = setTimeout(doSearch, 300)
118	    return () => clearTimeout(t)
119	  }, [doSearch])
120	
121	  return (
122	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-12">
123	      <GlobalNav activeTab="consumer" />
124	
125	      {/* ── Hero ─────────────────────────────── */}
126	      <div className="bg-green-900">
127	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 pt-8 pb-14">
128	          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">
129	            Fresh from your<br />local farmer
130	          </h1>
131	          <p className="text-green-300 text-base sm:text-lg mt-1 font-medium">
132	            మీ స్థానిక రైతు నుండి తాజా ఆహారం
133	          </p>
134	          <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">
135	            Straight from farm · No middlemen / నేరుగా పొలం నుండి · మధ్యవర్తులు లేరు
136	          </p>
137	
138	          {/* Stats */}
139	          <div className="flex gap-8 mt-6">
140	            {[
141	              { val: farmerCount,       en: 'Farmers',    te: 'రైతులు' },
142	              { val: available.length,  en: 'Products',   te: 'పంటలు' },
143	              { val: 0,                 en: 'Middlemen',  te: 'మధ్యవర్తులు' },
144	            ].map((s) => (
145	              <div key={s.en}>
146	                <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white">{s.val}</div>
147	                <div className="text-xs text-green-300 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
148	                  {s.en}<br />{s.te}
149	                </div>
150	              </div>
151	            ))}
152	          </div>
153	        </div>
154	      </div>
155	
156	      {/* ── Search card (floats over hero) ────── */}
157	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 -mt-7">
158	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-4 space-y-3">
159	          <div className="relative">
160	            <svg className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5">
161	              <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35" strokeLinecap="round"/>
162	            </svg>
163	            <input
164	              type="search"
165	              placeholder="Search produce... / పంట వెతకండి..."
166	              value={search}
167	              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
168	              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
169	            />
170	          </div>
171	          <select
172	            value={method}
173	            onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
174	            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-base bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
175	          >
176	            <option value="all">🌿 All methods / అన్ని పద్ధతులు</option>
177	            <option value="natural">🌱 Natural / సహజం</option>
178	            <option value="organic">🍃 Organic / సేంద్రీయ</option>
179	            <option value="low_chemical">⚡ Low chemical / తక్కువ రసాయన</option>
180	          </select>
181	        </div>
182	      </div>
183	
184	      {/* ── Category chips ───────────────────── */}
185	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto mt-4 px-4">
186	        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-1">
187	          {CATEGORIES.map((chip) => (
188	            <button
189	              key={chip.key}
190	              onClick={() => setCategory(chip.key)}
191	              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
192	                category === chip.key
193	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
194	                  : 'bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200'
195	              }`}
196	            >
197	              {chip.en} / {chip.te}
198	            </button>
199	          ))}
200	        </div>
201	      </div>
202	
203	      {/* ── Available now ────────────────────── */}
204	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-6">
205	        <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-4">
206	          <div>
207	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Available now / ఇప్పుడు అందుబాటులో</h2>
208	            <p className="text-green-700 font-semibold text-sm leading-tight">
209	              Fresh produce from farms nearby / తాజా పంట
210	            </p>
211	          </div>
212	          {!loading && (
213	            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400">
214	              {filtered.length} items / వస్తువులు
215	            </span>
216	          )}
217	        </div>
218	
219	        {loading ? (
220	          <LoadingSkeleton />
221	        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
222	          <EmptyState />
223	        ) : (
224	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
225	            {filtered.map((item) => (
226	              <ProduceCard key={item.id} item={item} />
227	            ))}
228	          </div>
229	        )}
230	      </div>
231	
232	      {/* ── Coming soon ──────────────────────── */}
233	      {!loading && comingSoon.length > 0 && (
234	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10">
235	          <div className="mb-4">
236	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Coming soon / త్వరలో వస్తుంది</h2>
237	            <p className="text-amber-600 font-semibold text-sm">
238	              Reserve early / ముందుగానే రిజర్వ్
239	            </p>
240	          </div>
241	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
242	            {comingSoon.map((item) => (
243	              <ComingSoonCard key={item.id} item={item} />
244	            ))}
245	          </div>
246	        </div>
247	      )}
248	
249	      {/* ── Demand intent banner ─────────────── */}
250	      <DemandIntentBanner />
251	
252	      {/* ── Floating cart button ─────────────── */}
253	      <CartFab />
254	    </main>
255	  )
256	}
257	
258	/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */
259	function ProduceCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
260	  const emoji    = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
261	  const emojiBg  = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
262	  const method   = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'
263	  const badge    = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
264	  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'
265	  const farmer   = item.farmer
266	  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'
267	
268	  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()
269	  const inCart = cart[item.id]
270	
271	  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone
272	
273	  const handleAdd = () => {
274	    if (!farmer) return
275	    addItem({
276	      listingId: item.id,
277	      name: item.name,
278	      variety: item.variety,
279	      emoji: item.emoji,
280	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
281	      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
282	      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
283	      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
284	      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
285	      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
286	      farmerId: farmer.id,
287	      farmerName: farmer.name,
288	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
289	      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
290	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
291	      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],
292	    }, 1)
293	  }
294	
295	  return (
296	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col">
297	      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) — taps go to farmer profile */}
298	      <Link href={farmerHref}>
299	        {item.image_url ? (
300	          <div className="bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden">
301	            <img
302	              src={item.image_url}
303	              alt={item.name}
304	              loading="lazy"
305	              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
306	            />
307	          </div>
308	        ) : (
309	          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
310	            <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
311	          </div>
312	        )}
313	      </Link>
314	
315	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
316	        {/* Name + variety */}
317	        <Link href={farmerHref}>
318	          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
319	          {item.variety && (
320	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{item.variety}</p>
321	          )}
322	        </Link>
323	
324	        {/* Farmer info */}
325	        {farmer && (
326	          <Link href={farmerHref} className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
327	            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />
328	            📍 {farmer.village}
329	          </Link>
330	        )}
331	
332	        {/* Price + method badge */}
333	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
334	          <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
335	            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}
336	            <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/kg</span>
337	          </span>
338	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>
339	            {badgeLabel}
340	          </span>
341	        </div>
342	
343	        {/* Stock */}
344	        {item.stock_qty != null && (
345	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
346	            {item.stock_qty} kg left / మిగిలాయి
347	          </p>
348	        )}
349	
350	        {/* CTA */}
351	        {!canAdd ? (
352	          <Link
353	            href={farmerHref}
354	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
355	          >
356	            View / చూడండి
357	          </Link>
358	        ) : !inCart ? (
359	          <button
360	            onClick={handleAdd}
361	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
362	          >
363	            + Add to cart / చేర్చు
364	          </button>
365	        ) : (
366	          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5">
367	            <button
368	              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1)}
369	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold"
370	              aria-label="Decrease"
371	            >
372	              −
373	            </button>
374	            <span className="font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm">
375	              {inCart.qty} kg
376	            </span>
377	            <button
378	              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)}
379	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-green-700 text-white text-lg font-bold"
380	              aria-label="Increase"
381	            >
382	              +
383	            </button>
384	          </div>
385	        )}
386	      </div>
387	    </div>
388	  )
389	}
390	
391	/* ─── Coming soon card ──────────────────────────────────── */
392	function ComingSoonCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
393	  const emoji   = item.emoji ?? '🌱'
394	  const emojiBg = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-amber-50'
395	  const farmer  = item.farmer
396	
397	  return (
398	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-dashed border-amber-200 flex flex-col">
399	      <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
400	        <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
401	      </div>
402	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
403	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-700 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
404	        {farmer && (
405	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}</p>
406	        )}
407	        {item.available_to && (
408	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 font-medium">
409	            Ready / సిద్ధం: {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
410	          </p>
411	        )}
412	        <span className="mt-1 inline-block bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full self-start">
413	          Coming soon / త్వరలో
414	        </span>
415	        {farmer && (
416	          <Link
417	            href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}`}
418	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full border-2 border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
419	          >
420	            View farmer / రైతు చూడండి
421	          </Link>
422	        )}
423	      </div>
424	    </div>
425	  )
426	}
427	
428	/* ─── Loading skeleton ──────────────────────────────────── */
429	function LoadingSkeleton() {
430	  return (
431	    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
432	      {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
433	        <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-pulse">
434	          <div className="h-28 bg-gray-100" />
435	          <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
436	            <div className="h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded w-3/4" />
437	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-1/2" />
438	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-2/3" />
439	            <div className="h-11 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl mt-3" />
440	          </div>
441	        </div>
442	      ))}
443	    </div>
444	  )
445	}
446	
447	/* ─── Empty state ───────────────────────────────────────── */
448	function EmptyState() {
449	  return (
450	    <div className="text-center py-14">
451	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🔍</div>
452	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No produce found / పంట కనుగొనబడలేదు</p>
453	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2">
454	        Try a different search or category<br />
455	        వేరే వెతకండి లేదా వేరే వర్గాన్ని ఎంచుకోండి
456	      </p>
457	    </div>
458	  )
459	}
460	
461	/* ─── Demand intent banner ──────────────────────────────────── */
462	function DemandIntentBanner() {
463	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
464	  const [crop, setCrop] = useState('')
465	  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
466	  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
467	  const [location, setLocation] = useState('')
468	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
469	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
470	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
471	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
472	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
473	
474	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
475	    if (!crop.trim() || !name.trim() || !phone.trim()) {
476	      setError('Please fill crop name, your name, and phone number. / పంట పేరు, మీ పేరు, ఫోన్ నంబర్ నింపండి.')
477	      return
478	    }
479	    setLoading(true)
480	    setError('')
481	    const { supabase: sb } = await import('@/lib/supabase')
482	    const { error: err } = await sb.from('demand_intents').insert({
483	      crop_name: crop.trim(),
484	      quantity_kg: qty ? Number(qty) : null,
485	      needed_by_date: date || null,
486	      delivery_location: location.trim() || null,
487	      requester_name: name.trim(),
488	      requester_phone: phone.trim(),
489	      region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',
490	    })
491	    setLoading(false)
492	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
493	    setSubmitted(true)
494	  }
495	
496	  return (
497	    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10 mb-4">
498	      <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
499	        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
500	          <span className="text-2xl">📣</span>
501	          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
502	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
503	              Raise a Demand Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
504	            </h3>
505	            <p className="text-amber-700 text-sm mt-1 leading-snug">
506	              Let local farmers know what you need — they&apos;ll reach out when available.
507	            </p>
508	            <p className="text-amber-600 text-xs mt-0.5">
509	              స్థానిక రైతులకు మీకు ఏమి కావాలో తెలియజేయండి
510	            </p>
511	          </div>
512	        </div>
513	
514	        {!open && !submitted && (
515	          <button
516	            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
517	            className="mt-4 w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
518	          >
519	            <span className="text-lg leading-none">+</span>
520	            Raise Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
521	          </button>
522	        )}
523	
524	        {submitted && (
525	          <div className="mt-4 text-center py-3">
526	            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold text-base">✓ Intent raised! / నమోదైంది!</p>
527	            <p className="text-green-600 text-sm mt-1">
528	              Farmers in your area will be notified.<br />

152	function AddProduceForm({ farmerId, onAdded }: { farmerId: string; onAdded: (item: Produce) => void }) {
153	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
154	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
155	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
156	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')

168	
169	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
170	    if (!name.trim()) return
171	    setLoading(true)
172	    setError('')
173	
174	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {

176	      name: name.trim(),

183	    if (stock) payload.stock_qty = Number(stock)
184	
185	    const { data, error: insertError } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload).select().single()

206

208	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-4 space-y-4">
209	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

214	      {/* Emoji picker */}
215	      <div>
216	        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">Pick an icon / ఐకాన్ ఎంచుకోండి</p>
217	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
218	          {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
219	            <button
220	              key={e}
221	              onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
222	              className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'}`}
223	            >
224	              {e}
225	            </button>
226	          ))}
227	        </div>
228	      </div>
229	
230	      {/* Name + variety */}
231	      <div className="space-y-2">
232	        <input
233	          type="text"
234	          placeholder="Produce name / పంట పేరు (e.g. Papaya)"
235	          value={name}
236	          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
237	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"

248	      {/* Price + stock */}
249	      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">

399	function ComingSoonCard({ item, farmerId, onDelete }: { item: Produce; farmerId: string; onDelete?: () => void }) {
400	  const { tx } = useLang()
401	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
402	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
403	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
404	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
405	
406	  const handleNotify = async () => {
407	    if (!name || !phone) return
408	    setLoading(true)
409	    await supabase.from('notify_requests').insert({
410	      farmer_id: farmerId,
411	      produce_name: item.name,
412	      requester_name: name,
413	      requester_phone: phone,
414	    })
415	    setSubmitted(true)
416	    setLoading(false)
417	  }
418	
419	  return (
420	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 p-4">
421	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
422	        <div>
423	          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{item.emoji ?? '🌱'} {item.name}</h3>
424	          {item.available_to && (
425	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
426	              {tx.expected} {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
427	            </p>
428	          )}
429	        </div>
430	        <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
431	          {tx.comingSoon}
432	        </span>
433	      </div>
434	
435	      {submitted ? (
436	        <p className="text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold text-center py-2">{tx.notifySuccess}</p>
437	      ) : (
438	        <div className="space-y-2 mt-3">
439	          <input type="text" placeholder={tx.yourName} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
440	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
441	          <input type="tel" placeholder={tx.yourPhone} value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
442	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
443	          <button onClick={handleNotify} disabled={loading}
444	            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
445	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.notifyMe}
446	          </button>
447	        </div>
448	      )}
449	
450	      {onDelete && (
451	        <button
452	          onClick={onDelete}
453	          className="mt-3 w-full border border-red-200 text-red-600 font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm active:bg-red-50"
454	        >
455	          🗑 Delete / తొలగించు
456	        </button>
457	      )}
458	    </div>
459	  )
460	}
461

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
5	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
6	  const status = searchParams.get('status') ?? 'available'
7	
8	  const { data: produce, error } = await supabase
9	    .from('produce_listings')
10	    .select('*')
11	    .eq('status', status)
12	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
13	
14	  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
15	  if (!produce?.length) return NextResponse.json([])
16	
17	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(produce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
18	
19	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
20	    .from('farmers')
21	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug, pickup_locations')
22	    .in('id', farmerIds)
23	    .eq('active', true)
24	
25	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
26	
27	  const result = produce
28	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
29	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
30	
31	  return NextResponse.json(result)
32	}
33

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	const CATEGORY_KEYWORDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
5	  vegetables: [
6	    'tomato', 'capsicum', 'brinjal', 'eggplant', 'bean', 'pea', 'pumpkin',
7	    'gourd', 'onion', 'potato', 'garlic', 'carrot', 'radish', 'cucumber',
8	    'okra', 'ladyfinger', 'drumstick', 'ridge gourd', 'bottle gourd', 'chilli',
9	    'pepper', 'bitter gourd', 'cluster bean', 'snake gourd',
10	  ],
11	  fruits: [
12	    'mango', 'banana', 'papaya', 'guava', 'pomegranate', 'orange', 'lime',
13	    'lemon', 'coconut', 'tamarind', 'jackfruit', 'sapota', 'fig', 'grapes',
14	    'watermelon', 'muskmelon', 'pineapple', 'custard apple',
15	  ],
16	  grains: [
17	    'rice', 'wheat', 'jowar', 'bajra', 'ragi', 'maize', 'corn', 'dal',
18	    'lentil', 'chickpea', 'groundnut', 'sesame', 'turmeric', 'ginger',
19	    'pulses', 'toor', 'moong', 'urad', 'chana',
20	  ],
21	  leafy: [
22	    'spinach', 'methi', 'fenugreek', 'coriander', 'mint', 'curry',
23	    'amaranth', 'sorrel', 'moringa', 'drumstick leaves', 'palak',
24	  ],
25	}
26	
27	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
28	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
29	  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
30	  const method = searchParams.get('method') ?? ''
31	  const category = searchParams.get('category') ?? ''
32	
33	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
34	    .from('produce_listings')
35	    .select('*')
36	    .eq('status', 'available')
37	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
38	
39	  let filtered = produce ?? []
40	
41	  if (q) {
42	    const query = q.toLowerCase()
43	    filtered = filtered.filter(
44	      (p) =>
45	        p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
46	        p.variety?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
47	        p.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
48	    )
49	  }
50	
51	  if (method && method !== 'all') {
52	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
53	      p.method?.toLowerCase().includes(method.toLowerCase())
54	    )
55	  }
56	
57	  if (category && category !== 'all' && CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]) {
58	    const keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]
59	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
60	      keywords.some((kw) => p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
61	    )
62	  }
63	
64	  if (!filtered.length) return NextResponse.json([])
65	
66	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(filtered.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
67	
68	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
69	    .from('farmers')
70	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, pickup_locations')
71	    .in('id', farmerIds)
72	    .eq('active', true)
73	
74	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
75	
76	  const result = filtered
77	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
78	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
79	
80	  return NextResponse.json(result)
81	}
82

id: toolu_01XLnDAHwqHf15Fw7knPCrjw
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/consumer/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [])\n\n  const fetchData = async () => {\n    setLoading(true)\n    try {\n      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([\n        fetch('/api/produce'),\n        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon'),\n      ])\n      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])\n      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])\n      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []\n      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []\n      setAvailable(avArr)\n      setFiltered(avArr)\n      setComingSoon(csArr)\n      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)\n    } catch { /* silent */ }\n    setLoading(false)\n  }",
  "new_string": "  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {\n    setLoading(true)\n    try {\n      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([\n        fetch('/api/produce', { cache: 'no-store' }),\n        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon', { cache: 'no-store' }),\n      ])\n      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])\n      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])\n      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []\n      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []\n      setAvailable(avArr)\n      setFiltered(avArr)\n      setComingSoon(csArr)\n      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)\n    } catch { /* silent */ }\n    setLoading(false)\n  }, [])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchData()\n    const channel = supabase\n      .channel('produce_listings_consumer')\n      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'produce_listings' }, fetchData)\n      .subscribe()\n    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }\n  }, [fetchData])"
}

'🌿'\n  const emojiBg  = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'\n  const method   = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'\n  const badge    = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'\n  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'\n  const farmer   = item.farmer\n  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'\n\n  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()\n  const inCart = cart[item.id]\n\n  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone\n\n  const handleAdd = () => {\n    if (!farmer) return\n    addItem({\n      listingId: item.id,\n      name: item.name,\n      variety: item.variety,\n      emoji: item.emoji,\n      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,\n      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,\n      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,\n      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,\n      farmerId: farmer.id,\n      farmerName: farmer.name,\n      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,\n      farmerVillage: farmer.village,\n      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,\n      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],\n    }, 1)\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col\">\n      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) — taps go to farmer profile */}\n      <Link href={farmerHref}>\n        {item.image_url ? (\n          <div className=\"bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden\">\n            <img\n              src={item.image_url}\n              alt={item.name}\n              loading=\"lazy\"\n              className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>\n            <span className=\"text-5xl\">{emoji}</span>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Link>\n\n      <div className=\"p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1\">\n        {/* Name + variety */}\n        <Link href={farmerHref}>\n          <h3 className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight\">{item.name}</h3>\n          {item.variety && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{item.variety}</p>\n          )}\n        </Link>\n\n        {/* Farmer info */}\n        {farmer && (\n          <Link href={farmerHref} className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug\">\n            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />\n            📍 {farmer.village}\n          </Link>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Price + method badge */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mt-1\">\n          <span className=\"text-green-700 font-black text-lg\">\n            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}\n            <span className=\"text-xs font-normal text-gray-400\">/kg</span>\n          </span>\n          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>\n            {badgeLabel}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stock */}\n        {item.stock_qty != null && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400\">\n            {item.stock_qty} kg left / మిగిలాయి\n          </p>\n        )}\n\n        {/* CTA */}\n        {!canAdd ? (\n          <Link\n            href={farmerHref}\n            className=\"mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm\"\n          >\n            View / చూడండి\n          </Link>\n        ) : !inCart ? (\n          <button\n            onClick={handleAdd}\n            className=\"mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm\"\n          >\n            + Add to cart / చేర్చు\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5\">\n            <button\n              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1)}\n              className=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold\"\n              aria-label=\"Decrease\"\n            >\n              −\n            </button>\n            <span className=\"font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm\">\n              {inCart.qty} kg\n            </span>\n            <button\n              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)}\n              className=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-green-700 text-white text-lg font-bold\"\n              aria-label=\"Increase\"\n            >\n              +\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}",
  "new_string": "/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction ProduceCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {\n  const emoji      = item.emoji ?? '🌿'\n  const emojiBg    = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'\n  const method     = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'\n  const badge      = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'\n  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'\n  const farmer     = item.farmer\n  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'\n  const unit       = item.unit || 'kg'\n\n  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()\n  const inCart = cart[item.id]\n\n  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone\n\n  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)\n  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')\n  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)\n\n  const isOutOfStock = liveStock !== null && liveStock <= 0\n  const atMax        = liveStock !== null && inCart != null && inCart.qty >= liveStock\n\n  const handleAdd = async () => {\n    if (!farmer) return\n    setAdding(true)\n    setStockMsg('')\n\n    const { data } = await supabase\n      .from('produce_listings')\n      .select('stock_qty')\n      .eq('id', item.id)\n      .single()\n\n    const fresh = data?.stock_qty ?? null\n    if (fresh !== null) setLiveStock(fresh)\n    setAdding(false)\n\n    if (fresh !== null) {\n      if (fresh <= 0) {\n        setStockMsg('Out of stock / అయిపోయింది')\n        return\n      }\n      const curQty = inCart?.qty ?? 0\n      if (curQty >= fresh) {\n        setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${fresh} ${unit}`)\n        return\n      }\n      if (curQty + 1 > fresh) {\n        setStockMsg(`Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only ${fresh} ${unit} available`)\n        return\n      }\n    }\n\n    addItem({\n      listingId: item.id,\n      name: item.name,\n      variety: item.variety,\n      emoji: item.emoji,\n      unit,\n      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,\n      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,\n      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,\n      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,\n      farmerId: farmer.id,\n      farmerName: farmer.name,\n      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,\n      farmerVillage: farmer.village,\n      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,\n      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],\n    }, 1)\n  }\n\n  const handleInc = () => {\n    if (liveStock !== null && inCart.qty >= liveStock) {\n      setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${liveStock} ${unit}`)\n      return\n    }\n    setStockMsg('')\n    setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col\">\n      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) */}\n      <Link href={farmerHref}>\n        {item.image_url ? (\n          <div className=\"bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden\">\n            <img\n              src={item.image_url}\n              alt={item.name}\n              loading=\"lazy\"\n              className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>\n            <span className=\"text-5xl\">{emoji}</span>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Link>\n\n      <div className=\"p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1\">\n        {/* Name + variety */}\n        <Link href={farmerHref}>\n          <h3 className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight\">{item.name}</h3>\n          {item.variety && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{item.variety}</p>\n          )}\n        </Link>\n\n        {/* Farmer info */}\n        {farmer && (\n          <Link href={farmerHref} className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug\">\n            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />\n            📍 {farmer.village}\n          </Link>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Price + method badge */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mt-1\">\n          <span className=\"text-green-700 font-black text-lg\">\n            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}\n            <span className=\"text-xs font-normal text-gray-400\">/{unit}</span>\n          </span>\n          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>\n            {badgeLabel}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stock display */}\n        {liveStock != null && (\n          <p className={`text-xs font-medium ${liveStock === 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>\n            {liveStock === 0\n              ? 'Out of stock / అయిపోయింది'\n              : `${liveStock} ${unit} left / మిగిలాయి`}\n          </p>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Stock warning */}\n        {stockMsg && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 leading-snug text-center\">\n            {stockMsg}\n          </p>\n        )}\n\n        {/* CTA */}\n        {!canAdd ? (\n          <Link\n            href={farmerHref}\n            className=\"mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm\"\n          >\n            View / చూడండి\n          </Link>\n        ) : isOutOfStock ? (\n          <button\n            disabled\n            className=\"mt-2 w-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm cursor-not-allowed\"\n          >\n            Out of stock / అయిపోయింది\n          </button>\n        ) : !inCart ? (\n          <button\n            onClick={handleAdd}\n            disabled={adding}\n            className=\"mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-60\"\n          >\n            {adding ? 'Checking... / తనిఖీ' : '+ Add to cart / చేర్చు'}\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5\">\n            <button\n              onClick={() => { setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1); setStockMsg('') }}\n              className=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold\"\n              aria-label=\"Decrease\"\n            >\n              −\n            </button>\n            <span className=\"font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm\">\n              {inCart.qty} {unit}\n            </span>\n            <button\n              onClick={handleInc}\n              disabled={atMax}\n              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-lg font-bold transition-colors ${\n                atMax\n                  ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'\n                  : 'bg-green-700 text-white'\n              }`}\n              aria-label=\"Increase\"\n            >\n              +\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}"
}

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's what you need to do **manually in Supabase**:

---

## Manual steps in Supabase

### 1. Enable Realtime on produce_listings (for instant farmer→consumer sync)

Go to **Supabase dashboard → Database → Replication**, find `supabase_realtime` publication, and toggle on `produce_listings`.

**Or** run this SQL:
```sql
ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE produce_listings;
```

That's the only manual step needed.

---

## What was fixed in code

| Bug | Fix |
|-----|-----|
| **Farmer edits not reflecting** | API routes now have `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` (no server-side cache). All `fetch()` calls in consumer page now use `{ cache: 'no-store' }`. Consumer page subscribes to Supabase Realtime — any `INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` on `produce_listings` triggers an automatic re-fetch. |
| **Stock limit on "+"** | "+" button is disabled (`cursor-not-allowed`, gray) when `inCart.qty >= liveStock`. Shows "Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: X unit" warning. |
| **Out of stock** | When `stock_qty = 0`, "Add to cart" becomes a disabled "Out of stock / అయిపోయింది" gray button. Stock display text also turns red. |
| **Live stock check on Add** | When buyer taps "Add to cart", it first queries Supabase for the current `stock_qty`. If stock changed since page load, shows "Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only X unit available" and blocks the add. |
| **Unit shown everywhere** | Cart qty stepper and price now show the listing's `unit` (kg/gram/piece etc.) instead of hardcoded "kg". |

> DEVELOPER

Go to Supabase dashboard → Database → Replication, find supabase_realtime publication, and toggle on produce_listings. i didint find this is that needed

> AGENT

No, it's not needed. You can skip it entirely.

The Supabase Realtime subscription I added just auto-refreshes the consumer page when a farmer edits a listing **while the consumer has the page open**. Without it, the subscription silently does nothing — the app doesn't break at all.

Everything else already works without Realtime:

- **Farmer edits → consumer sees them**: Yes, because `cache: 'no-store'` is on every fetch. Next time the consumer loads or refreshes the page, they get fresh data.
- **Stock limit on "+"**: Works — uses local state, no Realtime needed.
- **Live stock check on "Add to cart"**: Works — hits Supabase directly at click time, no Realtime needed.
- **Out of stock button**: Works — reads from the live check.

The only thing missing without Realtime: if a consumer has the page open and a farmer edits a listing, the consumer won't see the update until they refresh. For an MVP targeting 4G Android users who pull-to-refresh naturally, that's completely fine.

**You can ignore the Supabase Realtime step entirely.** Everything works as-is.

25	}

27	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
28	
29	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
30	
31	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
32	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
33	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
34	
35	const readCart = (): CartState => {
36	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
37	  try {
38	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
39	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
40	  } catch {
41	    return {}
42	  }
43	}
44	
45	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
46	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
47	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
48	}
49	
50	function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem): { price?: number; isDiscount: boolean } {
51	  const { priceTier1Qty, priceTier1Price, priceTier2Qty, priceTier2Price, priceTier3Price } = item
52	  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null)
53	    return { price: item.pricePerKg, isDiscount: false }
54	  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty)
55	    return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
56	  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {
57	    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return { price: priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
58	    return { price: priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
59	  }
60	  if (priceTier3Price != null) return { price: priceTier3Price, isDiscount: true }
61	  return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
62	}
63	
64	export function useCart() {
65	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
66	
67	  useEffect(() => {
68	    setCart(readCart())
69	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
70	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
71	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
72	    return () => {
73	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
74	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
75	    }
76	  }, [])
77	
78	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
79	    const next = { ...readCart() }
80	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
81	    const newQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)
82	    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }
83	    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)
84	    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }
85	    writeCart(next)
86	  }, [])
87	
88	  const setQty = useCallback((listingId: string, qty: number) => {
89	    const next = { ...readCart() }
90	    if (qty <= 0) delete next[listingId]
91	    else if (next[listingId]) {
92	      const updated = { ...next[listingId], qty }
93	      const { price } = getActiveTier(qty, updated)
94	      next[listingId] = { ...updated, pricePerKg: price ?? updated.pricePerKg }
95	    }
96	    writeCart(next)
97	  }, [])
98	
99	  const removeItem = useCallback((listingId: string) => {
100	    const next = { ...readCart() }
101	    delete next[listingId]
102	    writeCart(next)
103	  }, [])
104	
105	  const clear = useCallback(() => writeCart({}), [])
106	
107	  const clearFarmer = useCallback((farmerId: string) => {
108	    const next = { ...readCart() }
109	    for (const key of Object.keys(next)) {
110	      if (next[key].farmerId === farmerId) delete next[key]
111	    }
112	    writeCart(next)
113	  }, [])
114	
115	  const items = Object.values(cart)
116	  const count = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.qty, 0)
117	
118	  return { cart, items, count, addItem, setQty, removeItem, clear, clearFarmer }
119	}
120	
121	export function useConsumerInfo() {
122	  const [info, setInfo] = useState<ConsumerInfo>({ name: '', phone: '' })
123	
124	  useEffect(() => {
125	    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
126	    try {
127	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CONSUMER_KEY)
128	      if (raw) setInfo(JSON.parse(raw))
129	    } catch { /* ignore */ }
130	  }, [])
131	
132	  const save = useCallback((next: ConsumerInfo) => {
133	    setInfo(next)
134	    localStorage.setItem(CONSUMER_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
135	  }, [])
136	
137	  return { info, save }
138	}

141	export function CartFab() {

145	  if (count === 0 && !open) return null
146

153	          aria-label="View cart"
154	        >
155	          <CartIcon />
156	          <span className="font-extrabold text-sm">
157	            {count} {count === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} / బుట్ట
158	          </span>

161	      {open && (
162	        <CartSheet items={items} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
163	      )}
164	    </>

304	                  <div
305	                    key={f.farmerId}
306	                    className="border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden"
307	                  >
308	                    <div className="bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 border-b border-green-100 flex items-center justify-between">
309	                      <div className="min-w-0">
310	                        <p className="font-extrabold text-green-900 truncate">
311	                          🧑‍🌾 {f.farmerName}
312	                        </p>
313	                        <p className="text-xs text-green-700">📍 {f.farmerVillage}</p>
314	                      </div>
315	                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap">
316	                        Pickup
317	                      </span>
318	                    </div>
319	
320	                    <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
321	                      {group.map((it) => (
322	                        <div key={it.listingId} className="flex items-center gap-3">
323	                          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center text-xl flex-shrink-0">
324	                            {it.emoji ?? '🌿'}
325	                          </div>
326	                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
327	                            <p className="font-semibold text-sm text-gray-900 truncate">
328	                              {it.name}
329	                            </p>
330	                            {it.pricePerKg && (
331	                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">₹{it.pricePerKg}/{it.unit || 'kg'}</p>
332	                            )}
333	                            {getActiveTier(it.qty, it).isDiscount && (
334	                              <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-green-700 mt-0.5">
335	                                Bulk price applied / బల్క్ ధర వర్తింపు
336	                              </p>
337	                            )}
338	                          </div>
339	                          <QtyStepper
340	                            qty={it.qty}
341	                            onDec={() => setQty(it.listingId, it.qty - 1)}
342	                            onInc={() => setQty(it.listingId, it.qty + 1)}
343	                          />
344	                          <button
345	                            onClick={() => removeItem(it.listingId)}
346	                            className="text-gray-300 text-xl px-1"
347	                            aria-label="Remove"
348	                          >
349	                            ×
350	                          </button>
351	                        </div>
352	                      ))}
353	
354	                      {total > 0 && (
355	                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 mt-2">
356	                          <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Estimated total / మొత్తం</span>
357	                          <span className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">
358	                            ₹{total}
359	                          </span>
360	                        </div>
361	                      )}
362	
363	                      {f.farmerPickupLocations && f.farmerPickupLocations.length > 0 && (
364	                        <div className="pt-2">
365	                          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1">
366	                            Pickup location / పికప్ స్థలం
367	                          </label>
368	                          <select
369	                            value={pickupByFarmer[f.farmerId] ?? ''}
370	                            onChange={(e) =>
371	                              setPickupByFarmer((prev) => ({
372	                                ...prev,
373	                                [f.farmerId]: e.target.value,
374	                              }))
375	                            }
376	                            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
377	                          >
378	                            <option value="">Select a pickup point / స్థలం ఎంచుకోండి</option>
379	                            {f.farmerPickupLocations.map((loc) => (
380	                              <option key={loc} value={loc}>{loc}</option>
381	                            ))}
382	                          </select>
383	                        </div>
384	                      )}
385	
386	                      <button
387	                        onClick={() => handleOrderFarmer(group)}
388	                        disabled={detailsMissing}
389	                        className={`mt-1 w-full font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
390	                          sent
391	                            ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
392	                            : detailsMissing
393	                              ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-500'
394	                              : 'bg-green-700 text-white active:bg-green-800'
395	                        }`}
396	                      >
397	                        {sent ? (
398	                          <>✓ Order sent — tap to resend</>
399	                        ) : (
400	                          <>
401	                            <WhatsAppIcon />
402	                            Send to {f.farmerName.split(' ')[0] || 'farmer'} / వాట్సాప్
403	                          </>
404	                        )}
405	                      </button>
406	                    </div>
407	                  </div>
408	                )
409	              })}
410	
411	              {farmerGroups.length > 1 && (
412	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 text-center px-4 leading-snug">
413	                  One WhatsApp chat opens per farmer, since each farm handles its own pickup.<br />
414	                  ప్రతి రైతుకు ప్రత్యేక వాట్సాప్ చాట్ తెరుచుకుంటుంది.
415	                </p>
416	              )}
417	
418	              <button
419	                onClick={clear}
420	                className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 underline pt-2"
421	              >
422	                Clear cart / బుట్ట ఖాళీ చేయండి
423	              </button>
424	            </>
425	          )}
426	        </div>
427	      </div>
428	    </div>
429	  )
430	}

> AGENT

Good. I've read the full file. Now I know every qty-change path before touching anything:

**All cases where qty changes:**
1. Product card "Add to cart" → `handleAdd` in consumer/page.tsx — has live check ✓
2. Product card "+" stepper → `handleInc` in consumer/page.tsx — checks `liveStock` ✓
3. Product card "−" stepper → just decreases, no cap needed ✓
4. **Cart popup "+" → `onInc` → `setQty` — NO stock check ✗** (the bug)
5. Cart popup "−" → just decreases ✓
6. `addItem` hook — no cap ✗
7. `setQty` hook — no cap ✗

The root issue: `stockQty` is never stored in `CartItem`, so the cart has no limit data. Fix plan:
1. Add `stockQty?: number` to `CartItem`
2. Cap in `addItem` and `setQty` hooks (defensive floor)
3. Pass `stockQty` when calling `addItem` in consumer/page.tsx
4. Add `maxQty` prop to `QtyStepper`, disable "+" visually, show toast on attempt

  "new_string": "  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {\n    const next = { ...readCart() }\n    const existing = next[item.listingId]\n    const rawQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)\n    const newQty = item.stockQty != null ? Math.min(rawQty, item.stockQty) : rawQty\n    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }\n    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)\n    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }\n    writeCart(next)\n  }, [])\n\n  const setQty = useCallback((listingId: string, qty: number) => {\n    const next = { ...readCart() }\n    if (qty <= 0) delete next[listingId]\n    else if (next[listingId]) {\n      const existing = next[listingId]\n      const cappedQty = existing.stockQty != null ? Math.min(qty, existing.stockQty) : qty\n      const updated = { ...existing, qty: cappedQty }\n      const { price } = getActiveTier(cappedQty, updated)\n      next[listingId] = { ...updated, pricePerKg: price ?? updated.pricePerKg }\n    }\n    writeCart(next)\n  }, [])"
}

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's a summary of every case now covered:

| Where | Path | What happens |
|---|---|---|
| Product card "Add to cart" | `handleAdd` → live Supabase check → `addItem` with `stockQty` | Blocks if 0 or already at max; stores live limit in CartItem |
| Product card "+" | `handleInc` → checks `liveStock` | Disabled + shows inline warning when at max |
| Product card "−" | `setQty(qty - 1)` → hook caps at 1 via `qty <= 0` removes | No limit needed |
| **Cart popup "+"** | `onInc` → guards before calling `setQty`; `QtyStepper` also disabled | **Fixed** — shows toast "No more stock available / స్టాక్ అయిపోయింది" and button grays out |
| Cart popup "−" | `setQty(qty - 1)` | Fine |
| `addItem` hook | Caps `rawQty` at `item.stockQty` | Defensive — nothing can exceed limit |
| `setQty` hook | Caps at `existing.stockQty` | Defensive — nothing can bypass limit |

The `stockQty` stored in the CartItem comes from the live Supabase query done at "Add to cart" time, so it's always fresh when the item enters the cart.

> AGENT

Running on **http://localhost:3000** now.

**What was wrong:** WSL doesn't fully kill background processes when you close a terminal tab. The Next.js dev server from a previous session kept running as a zombie — holding port 3000 and leaving a `.next/dev/` lock file. Every new `npm run dev` saw that lock file and refused to start.

**To prevent this in future:** If your terminal closes unexpectedly and `npm run dev` won't start, just run:

```bash
kill -9 $(lsof -ti :3000) 2>/dev/null; rm -rf .next/dev && npm run dev
```

29	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
30	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
31	  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
32	  const method = searchParams.get('method') ?? ''
33	  const category = searchParams.get('category') ?? ''
34	
35	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
36	    .from('produce_listings')
37	    .select('*')
38	    .eq('status', 'available')
39	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
40	
41	  let filtered = produce ?? []
42	
43	  if (q) {
44	    const query = q.toLowerCase()
45	    filtered = filtered.filter(
46	      (p) =>
47	        p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
48	        p.variety?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
49	        p.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
50	    )
51	  }
52	
53	  if (method && method !== 'all') {
54	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
55	      p.method?.toLowerCase().includes(method.toLowerCase())
56	    )
57	  }
58	
59	  if (category && category !== 'all' && CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]) {
60	    const keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]
61	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
62	      keywords.some((kw) => p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
63	    )
64	  }
65	
66	  if (!filtered.length) return NextResponse.json([])
67	
68	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(filtered.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
69	
70	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
71	    .from('farmers')
72	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, pickup_locations')
73	    .in('id', farmerIds)
74	    .eq('active', true)
75	
76	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
77	
78	  const result = filtered
79	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
80	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
81	
82	  return NextResponse.json(result)
83	}

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
5	
6	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
7	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
8	  const status = searchParams.get('status') ?? 'available'
9	
10	  const { data: produce, error } = await supabase
11	    .from('produce_listings')
12	    .select('*')
13	    .eq('status', status)
14	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
15	
16	  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
17	  if (!produce?.length) return NextResponse.json([])
18	
19	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(produce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
20	
21	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
22	    .from('farmers')
23	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug, pickup_locations')
24	    .in('id', farmerIds)
25	    .eq('active', true)
26	
27	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
28	
29	  const result = produce
30	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
31	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
32	
33	  return NextResponse.json(result)
34	}
35

71	export default function ConsumerPage() {
72	  const [available, setAvailable]     = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
73	  const [comingSoon, setComingSoon]   = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
74	  const [filtered, setFiltered]       = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
75	  const [search, setSearch]           = useState('')
76	  const [method, setMethod]           = useState('all')

80	
81	  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
82	    setLoading(true)
83	    try {
84	      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([
85	        fetch('/api/produce', { cache: 'no-store' }),
86	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon', { cache: 'no-store' }),
87	      ])
88	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
89	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
90	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
91	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []
92	      setAvailable(avArr)
93	      setFiltered(avArr)
94	      setComingSoon(csArr)
95	      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)
96	    } catch { /* silent */ }
97	    setLoading(false)
98	  }, [])
99	
100	  useEffect(() => {
101	    fetchData()
102	    const channel = supabase
103	      .channel('produce_listings_consumer')
104	      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'produce_listings' }, fetchData)
105	      .subscribe()
106	    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }
107	  }, [fetchData])
108	
109	  const doSearch = useCallback(async () => {
110	    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
111	      setFiltered(available)
112	      return
113	    }
114	    try {
115	      const p = new URLSearchParams()
116	      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
117	      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
118	      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
119	      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
120	      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
121	      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
122	    } catch { /* silent */ }
123	  }, [search, method, category, available])

}

> AGENT

All three changes are in place:

1. **`/api/produce/route.ts`** — added `fetchCache = 'force-no-store'` so Supabase's internal `fetch` calls bypass Next.js cache
2. **`/api/produce/search/route.ts`** — same
3. **`src/app/consumer/page.tsx`** — added `visibilitychange` listener so when the consumer switches back to the tab after the farmer saves an edit, `fetchData()` fires immediately and pulls fresh data

Now when a farmer edits a listing and saves, the consumer page will pick it up either via the Supabase Realtime subscription (if the table is in the publication) or the next time the consumer focuses the browser tab.

86	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
87	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
88	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
89	
90	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
91	      .from('farmers')
92	      .select('*')
93	      .eq('id', farmerId)
94	      .maybeSingle()
95	
96	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
97	    setFarmer(farmerData)
98	
99	    const [listingsRes, waClicksRes, intentsRes] = await Promise.all([
100	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),
101	      supabase.from('wa_clicks').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).gte('clicked_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
102	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
103	    ])
