330	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */
331	function ProfileEditModal({
332	  farmer,
333	  onClose,
334	  onSaved,
335	}: {
336	  farmer: Farmer
337	  onClose: () => void
338	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
339	}) {
340	  const { tx } = useLang()
341	  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')
342	  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')
343	  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')
344	  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')
345	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
346	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
347	  )
348	  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(
349	    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],
350	  )
351	  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')
352	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
353	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
354	
355	  const addPickup = () => {
356	    const v = newPickup.trim()
357	    if (!v) return
358	    if (pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }
359	    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v])
360	    setNewPickup('')
361	  }
362	  const removePickup = (loc: string) =>
363	    setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))
364	
365	  const handleSave = async () => {
366	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.nameRequired); return }
367	    if (!village.trim()) { setError(tx.villageRequired); return }
368	    setLoading(true)
369	    setError('')
370	
371	    // Build a unique slug from the name once the farmer actually has one.
372	    // Only regenerate slug if current slug still looks auto-generated (f-<digits>-xxxx).
373	    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')
374	    const baseSlug = name
375	      .toLowerCase()
376	      .trim()
377	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
378	      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
379	      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'
380	
381	    let newSlug = farmer.slug
382	    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {
383	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)
384	      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`
385	    }
386	
387	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
388	      name: name.trim(),
389	      village: village.trim(),
390	      district: district.trim(),
391	      method,
392	      slug: newSlug,
393	      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,
394	    }
395	    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)
396	
397	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
398	      .from('farmers')
399	      .update(payload)
400	      .eq('id', farmer.id)
401	      .select('*')
402	      .single()
403	
404	    setLoading(false)
405	
406	    if (err || !data) {
407	      setError(err?.message ?? tx.couldNotSave)
408	      return
409	    }
410	
411	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)
412	    onSaved(data)
413	  }
414	
415	  return (
416	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
417	      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
418	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
419	          <div>
420	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">
421	              {tx.profileModalTitle}
422	            </h3>
423	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{tx.profileModalSubtitle}</p>
424	          </div>
425	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
426	        </div>
427	
428	        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
429	          <Field
430	            label={tx.yourNameLabel}
431	            placeholder="Ramu Reddy"
432	            value={name}
433	            onChange={setName}
434	          />
435	          <Field
436	            label={tx.villageLabel}
437	            placeholder="Tadepalligudem"
438	            value={village}
439	            onChange={setVillage}
440	          />
441	          <Field
442	            label={tx.districtLabel}
443	            placeholder="West Godavari"
444	            value={district}
445	            onChange={setDistrict}
446	          />
447	
448	          <div>
449	            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
450	              {tx.farmingMethodLabel}
451	            </label>
452	            <select
453	              value={method}
454	              onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
455	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
456	            >
457	              <option value="natural">{tx.methodNatural}</option>
458	              <option value="organic">{tx.methodOrganic}</option>
459	              <option value="low_chemical">{tx.methodLowChemical}</option>
460	              <option value="chemical">{tx.methodChemical}</option>
461	            </select>
462	          </div>
463	
464	          <Field
465	            label={tx.farmingSinceLabel}
466	            placeholder="e.g. 2005"
467	            value={sinceYear}
468	            onChange={setSinceYear}
469	            type="number"
470	          />
471	
472	          <div>
473	            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
474	              {tx.pickupLocationsLabel}
475	            </label>
476	            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2 leading-snug">
477	              {tx.pickupLocationsHelp}
478	            </p>
479	            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
480	              <input
481	                type="text"
482	                placeholder={tx.pickupPlaceholder}
483	                value={newPickup}
484	                onChange={(e) => setNewPickup(e.target.value)}
485	                onKeyDown={(e) => {
486	                  if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); addPickup() }
487	                }}
488	                className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
489	              />
490	              <button
491	                type="button"
492	                onClick={addPickup}
493	                className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-4 rounded-xl text-sm"
494	              >
495	                {tx.addBtn}
496	              </button>
497	            </div>
498	            {pickupLocations.length > 0 && (
499	              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
500	                {pickupLocations.map((loc) => (
501	                  <span
502	                    key={loc}
503	                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 text-green-800 rounded-full pl-3 pr-1 py-1 text-xs font-semibold"
504	                  >
505	                    📍 {loc}
506	                    <button
507	                      type="button"
508	                      onClick={() => removePickup(loc)}
509	                      className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-green-200 text-green-800 text-sm leading-none flex items-center justify-center"
510	                      aria-label={`Remove ${loc}`}
511	                    >
512	                      ×
513	                    </button>
514	                  </span>
515	                ))}
516	              </div>
517	            )}
518	          </div>
519	
520	          {error && (
521	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
522	          )}
523	
524	          <button
525	            onClick={handleSave}
526	            disabled={loading || !name.trim() || !village.trim()}
527	            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800"
528	          >
529	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.saveProfile}
530	          </button>
531	        </div>
532	      </div>
533	    </div>
534	  )
535	}
536	
537	function Field({
538	  label,
539	  placeholder,
540	  value,
541	  onChange,
542	  type = 'text',
543	}: {
544	  label: string
545	  placeholder?: string
546	  value: string
547	  onChange: (v: string) => void
548	  type?: string
549	}) {
550	  return (
551	    <div>
552	      <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
553	        {label}
554	      </label>
555	      <input
556	        type={type}
557	        placeholder={placeholder}
558	        value={value}
559	        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
560	        className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
561	      />
562	    </div>
563	  )
564	}
565	
566	/* ─── Produce listing form ──────────────────────────────────── */
567	function ProduceListingForm({
568	  farmerId,
569	  farmerSlug = '',
570	  defaultMethod,
571	  editData,
572	  onClose,
573	  onPublished,
574	}: {
575	  farmerId: string
576	  farmerSlug?: string
577	  defaultMethod: string
578	  editData?: ListingRow | null
579	  onClose: () => void
580	  onPublished: () => void
581	}) {
582	  const { tx } = useLang()
583	  const isEdit = !!editData
584	  const [name, setName] = useState(editData?.name ?? '')
585	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState(editData?.variety ?? '')
586	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState(editData?.emoji ?? '🌿')
587	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(editData?.stock_qty != null ? String(editData.stock_qty) : '')
588	  const [period, setPeriod] = useState('')
589	  const [farmingMethod, setFarmingMethod] = useState(editData?.method ?? defaultMethod ?? 'natural')
590	  const [price1, setPrice1] = useState(editData?.price_tier_1_price != null ? String(editData.price_tier_1_price) : '')
591	  const [price1Qty, setPrice1Qty] = useState(editData?.price_tier_1_qty != null ? String(editData.price_tier_1_qty) : '5')
592	  const [price2, setPrice2] = useState(editData?.price_tier_2_price != null ? String(editData.price_tier_2_price) : '')
593	  const [price2Qty, setPrice2Qty] = useState(editData?.price_tier_2_qty != null ? String(editData.price_tier_2_qty) : '20')
594	  const [price3, setPrice3] = useState(editData?.price_tier_3_price != null ? String(editData.price_tier_3_price) : '')
595	  const [description, setDescription] = useState(editData?.description ?? '')
596	  const [brix, setBrix] = useState(editData?.brix != null ? String(editData.brix) : '')
597	  const [soc, setSoc] = useState(editData?.soil_organic_carbon != null ? String(editData.soil_organic_carbon) : '')
598	  const [unit, setUnit] = useState(editData?.unit ?? 'kg')
599	  const [imageFile, setImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
600	  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string>('')
601	  const [existingImageUrl, setExistingImageUrl] = useState(editData?.image_url ?? '')
602	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
603	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
604	  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(false)
605	  const [published, setPublished] = useState(false)
606	  const [publishedSlug, setPublishedSlug] = useState('')
607	
608	  const handlePickImage = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
609	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
610	    if (!file) return
611	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
612	      setError(tx.pickImageFile)
613	      return
614	    }
615	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) {
616	      setError(tx.imageTooLarge)
617	      return
618	    }
619	    setError('')
620	    if (imagePreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview)
621	    setImageFile(file)
622	    setImagePreview(URL.createObjectURL(file))
623	  }
624	
625	  const clearImage = () => {
626	    if (imagePreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview)
627	    setImageFile(null)
628	    setImagePreview('')
629	    setExistingImageUrl('')
630	  }
631	
632	  const uploadImage = async (file: File): Promise<string | null> => {
633	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
634	    const path = `${farmerId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}.${ext}`
635	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
636	      .from('farm-images')
637	      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
638	    if (upErr) {
639	      setError(`Image upload failed: ${upErr.message}`)
640	      return null
641	    }
642	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
643	    return data.publicUrl
644	  }
645	
646	  const previewData: PreviewData = {
647	    name: name || 'Produce name',
648	    variety: variety || '',
649	    emoji,
650	    price: price1 || '—',
651	    method: farmingMethod,
652	    stock: qty || '—',
653	  }
654	
655	  const handlePublish = async () => {
656	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.produceNameRequired); return }
657	    setLoading(true)
658	    setError('')
659	
660	    let imageUrl: string | null = null
661	    if (imageFile) {
662	      imageUrl = await uploadImage(imageFile)
663	      if (!imageUrl) { setLoading(false); return }
664	    } else if (existingImageUrl) {
665	      imageUrl = existingImageUrl
666	    }
667	
668	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
669	      name: name.trim(),
670	      emoji,
671	      method: farmingMethod,
672	      unit,
673	    }
674	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
675	    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)
676	    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()
677	    else payload.description = null
678	    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)
679	    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)
680	    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }
681	    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }
682	    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }
683	    payload.image_url = imageUrl
684	
685	    if (isEdit && editData) {
686	      const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').update(payload).eq('id', editData.id)
687	      setLoading(false)
688	      if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
689	      onPublished()
690	      return
691	    }
692	
693	    const insertPayload = { ...payload, farmer_id: farmerId, status: 'available' }
694	    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(insertPayload)
695	    setLoading(false)
696	
697	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
698	
699	    setPublished(true)
700	    setPublishedSlug(farmerSlug)
701	    setTimeout(() => onPublished(), 2500)
702	  }
703	
704	  if (published) {
705	    return (
706	      <div className="bg-green-50 border-2 border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center space-y-3">
707	        <div className="text-4xl">✅</div>
708	        <p className="font-extrabold text-green-800 text-lg">{tx.publishedTitle}</p>
709	        <p className="text-green-700 text-sm">{tx.listingLive}</p>
710	        <Link
711	          href={`/farmer/${publishedSlug}`}
712	          className="inline-block bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
713	        >
714	          {tx.viewYourProfile} ↗
715	        </Link>
716	      </div>
717	    )
718	  }
719	
720	  return (
721	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
722	      {/* Form header */}
723	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
724	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">
725	          {isEdit ? tx.editProduceListing : tx.newProduceListing}
726	        </h3>
727	        <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
728	      </div>
729	
730	      <div className="p-4 space-y-5">
731	        {/* Emoji picker */}
732	        <div>
733	          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-2">{tx.pickIcon}</p>
734	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
735	            {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
736	              <button
737	                key={e}
738	                onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
739	                className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
740	                  emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'
741	                }`}
742	              >
743	                {e}
744	              </button>
745	            ))}
746	          </div>
747	        </div>
748	
749	        {/* Unit selector */}
750	        <div className="space-y-2">
751	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
752	            Unit / కొలత
753	          </label>
754	          <select
755	            value={unit}
756	            onChange={(e) => setUnit(e.target.value)}
757	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
758	          >
759	            {UNIT_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
760	              <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>
761	            ))}
762	          </select>
763	        </div>
764	
765	        {/* Name + variety */}
766	        <div className="space-y-2">
767	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
768	            {tx.produceDetails}
769	          </label>
770	          <input
771	            type="text"
772	            placeholder={tx.produceNamePlaceholder}
773	            value={name}
774	            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
775	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
776	          />
777	          <input
778	            type="text"
779	            placeholder={tx.varietyPlaceholder}
780	            value={variety}
781	            onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
782	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
783	          />
784	        </div>
785	
786	        {/* Qty + period */}
787	        <div className="space-y-2">
788	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
789	            {tx.availability}
790	          </label>
791	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
792	            <input
793	              type="number"
794	              placeholder={`Quantity (${unit})`}
795	              value={qty}
796	              onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
797	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
798	            />
799	            <input
800	              type="text"
801	              placeholder={tx.periodPlaceholder}
802	              value={period}
803	              onChange={(e) => setPeriod(e.target.value)}
804	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
805	            />
806	          </div>
807	        </div>
808	
809	        {/* Farming method */}
810	        <div className="space-y-2">
811	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
812	            {tx.farmingMethodLabel}
813	          </label>
814	          <select
815	            value={farmingMethod}
816	            onChange={(e) => setFarmingMethod(e.target.value)}
817	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
818	          >
819	            <option value="natural">{tx.methodNatural}</option>
820	            <option value="organic">{tx.methodOrganic}</option>
821	            <option value="low_chemical">{tx.methodLowChemical}</option>
822	            <option value="chemical">{tx.methodChemical}</option>
823	          </select>
824	        </div>
825	
826	        {/* Delivery method */}
827	        <div className="space-y-2">
828	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
829	            {tx.deliveryMethod}
830	          </label>
831	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
832	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-2 border-green-600 bg-green-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-3">
833	              <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-green-600 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
834	                <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-white rounded-full" />
835	              </div>
836	              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-green-800">{tx.pickupOnly}</span>
837	            </div>
838	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed">
839	              <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-300 flex-shrink-0" />
840	              <div>
841	                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-400">{tx.courierOption}</span>
842	                <span className="block text-[10px] text-gray-400">{tx.courierComingSoon}</span>
843	              </div>
844	            </div>
845	          </div>
846	        </div>
847	
848	        {/* Pricing tiers */}
849	        <div className="space-y-2">
850	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
851	            {tx.pricingTiers}
852	          </label>
853	          <div className="space-y-2">
854	            <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
855	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 w-14 flex-shrink-0">Tier 1</span>
856	              <input
857	                type="number"
858	                placeholder={`Up to ${price1Qty}`}
859	                value={price1Qty}
860	                onChange={(e) => setPrice1Qty(e.target.value)}
861	                className="w-20 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 text-sm"
862	              />
863	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{unit} →</span>
864	              <div className="flex-1 relative">
865	                <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500 text-sm">₹</span>
866	                <input
867	                  type="number"
868	                  placeholder={`Price/${unit}`}
869	                  value={price1}
870	                  onChange={(e) => setPrice1(e.target.value)}
871	                  className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg pl-7 pr-3 py-2 text-sm"
872	                />
873	              </div>
874	            </div>
875	            <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
876	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 w-14 flex-shrink-0">Tier 2</span>
877	              <input
878	                type="number"
879	                placeholder={`Up to`}
880	                value={price2Qty}
881	                onChange={(e) => setPrice2Qty(e.target.value)}
882	                className="w-20 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 text-sm"
883	              />
884	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{unit} →</span>
885	              <div className="flex-1 relative">
886	                <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500 text-sm">₹</span>
887	                <input
888	                  type="number"
889	                  placeholder={`Price/${unit}`}
890	                  value={price2}
891	                  onChange={(e) => setPrice2(e.target.value)}
892	                  className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg pl-7 pr-3 py-2 text-sm"
893	                />
894	              </div>
895	            </div>
896	            <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
897	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 w-14 flex-shrink-0">Tier 3</span>
898	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 w-20 text-center">{Number(price2Qty) + 1}+ {unit}</span>
899	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">→</span>
900	              <div className="flex-1 relative">
901	                <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500 text-sm">₹</span>
902	                <input
903	                  type="number"
904	                  placeholder={`Price/${unit}`}
905	                  value={price3}
906	                  onChange={(e) => setPrice3(e.target.value)}
907	                  className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg pl-7 pr-3 py-2 text-sm"
908	                />
909	              </div>
910	            </div>
911	          </div>
912	        </div>
913	
914	        {/* Description */}
915	        <div className="space-y-2">
916	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
917	            {tx.description}
918	          </label>
919	          <textarea
920	            placeholder={tx.descriptionPlaceholder}
921	            value={description}
922	            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value.slice(0, 500))}
923	            rows={3}
924	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm resize-none focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
925	          />
926	          <p className="text-right text-xs text-gray-400">{description.length}/500</p>
927	        </div>
928	
929	        {/* Produce photo */}
930	        <div className="space-y-2">
931	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">
932	            {tx.photoOptional}
933	          </label>
934	          {(imagePreview || existingImageUrl) ? (
935	            <div className="relative">
936	              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
937	              <img
938	                src={imagePreview || existingImageUrl}
939	                alt="Preview"
940	                className="w-full max-h-64 object-cover rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50"
941	              />
942	              <button
943	                type="button"
944	                onClick={clearImage}
945	                className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 text-gray-700 rounded-full w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center text-base font-bold shadow"
946	                aria-label="Remove photo"
947	              >
948	                ×
949	              </button>
950	            </div>
951	          ) : (
952	            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
953	              <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-3 text-green-700 text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50">
954	                <span className="text-lg leading-none">📷</span>
955	                {tx.takePhoto}
956	                <input
957	                  type="file"
958	                  accept="image/*"
959	                  capture="environment"
960	                  onChange={handlePickImage}
961	                  className="hidden"
962	                />
963	              </label>
964	              <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-3 text-green-700 text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50">
965	                <span className="text-lg leading-none">🖼</span>
966	                {tx.fromGallery}
967	                <input
968	                  type="file"
969	                  accept="image/*"
970	                  onChange={handlePickImage}
971	                  className="hidden"
972	                />
973	              </label>
974	            </div>
975	          )}
976	          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
977	            {tx.buyersSeeCard}
978	          </p>
979	        </div>
980	
981	        {/* Quality params */}
982	        <div className="space-y-2">
983	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">

> AGENT

Here's what I understand after reading the full code:

**The edit form is wired up correctly.** `ProduceListingForm` opens pre-filled from `editData`, collects changes, and `handlePublish` does call `supabase.from('produce_listings').update(payload).eq('id', editData.id)`. The form then calls `onPublished()` which closes the overlay and calls `load()` to re-fetch listings.

**But the update is silently failing with 0 rows changed.** The code only does this:

```typescript
const { error: err } = await supabase...update(payload).eq('id', editData.id)
if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
onPublished()  // ← called even if 0 rows were actually updated
```

It checks for an error but **not whether any rows were actually modified**. Supabase Row Level Security (RLS), when it blocks a write, returns `{ data: [], error: null }` — no error, zero rows changed. The code sees no error → calls `onPublished()` → form closes → `load()` re-fetches the **unchanged** data from Supabase → manage modal shows old values → consumer page also shows old values because the DB was never actually modified.

**Root cause:** The app stores farmer ID in `localStorage` (no Supabase Auth session). The Supabase client uses the anon key with no `auth.uid()`. If `produce_listings` has an RLS UPDATE policy like `auth.uid() = farmer_id`, every update from the client will be silently blocked.

**The fix:** Move the update to a server-side API route (`/api/farmer/update-listing`) that uses `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`. The server bypasses RLS entirely. The client sends `{ listingId, farmerId, payload }`, the server verifies `farmerId` owns that listing, then does the update with the service role key.

Should I go ahead and implement this?

> AGENT

You need to do **one external step** — I can do everything else.

**What you need to do:**
1. Go to your Supabase dashboard → **Project Settings** → **API**
2. Copy the **`service_role`** secret key (the long one under "Project API keys")
3. Open `.env.local` and add this line:
   ```
   SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=your_key_here
   ```

**Why:** Your `.env.local` only has the anon key. The anon key respects Supabase Row Level Security (RLS), which is silently blocking updates from unauthenticated clients (your app uses localStorage, not Supabase Auth). The service role key bypasses RLS on the server side — I'll create a server API route that uses it to do the update safely.

Once you add that key, I'll:
- Create `/api/farmer/update-listing` — a server route that verifies the listing belongs to that farmer, then saves using the service role key
- Update the edit form to call that route instead of Supabase directly
- No Supabase dashboard changes needed beyond that

Add the key and let me know when done.

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/farmer/update-listing/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst supabaseAdmin = createClient(\n  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n)\n\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null)\n  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const { listingId, farmerId, payload } = body\n\n  if (!listingId || !farmerId || !payload) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing fields' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  // Verify this listing belongs to the farmer before updating\n  const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin\n    .from('produce_listings')\n    .select('id')\n    .eq('id', listingId)\n    .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!existing) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Listing not found or access denied' }, { status: 403 })\n  }\n\n  const { error } = await supabaseAdmin\n    .from('produce_listings')\n    .update(payload)\n    .eq('id', listingId)\n\n  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n}\n"
}

No errors. Here's what was done:

1. **Created `/api/farmer/update-listing`** — a server-side POST route that uses `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (bypasses RLS entirely). It also verifies the listing actually belongs to the farmer before updating, so it's safe.

2. **Updated `handlePublish` in edit mode** — instead of calling `supabase.update()` directly from the browser (which RLS was silently blocking), it now calls the server route. If the update fails for any reason, the error message is shown in the form instead of silently doing nothing.

Go ahead and test: open the farmer dashboard → Manage → Edit a listing → change qty/price → Save Changes. The changes should now persist and show immediately in both the manage modal and the consumer page.

> AGENT

Clean. Here's what changed:

**Edit mode** now sends every field in the payload — including `null` for anything the farmer clears. So:
- Change the name → saves
- Change emoji, unit, method → saves
- Clear a price tier → sets it to `null` in DB (removes it)
- Clear stock qty → sets to `null`
- Change description, brix, soil carbon → saves
- Change/remove photo → saves

**Insert mode** is unchanged — only sends fields that have values (avoids null constraint issues on new rows).

Test it: go to Manage → Edit → change name, emoji, any price, the qty → Save Changes. All of it should persist now.

Here's the actual bug, clearly:

**Root cause — two layers:**

1. **The DB save was silently blocked by RLS.** Your app stores farmer ID in `localStorage` — there's no Supabase Auth session. The Supabase anon key respects Row Level Security. RLS saw an unauthenticated request and blocked the UPDATE, but returned `{ error: null }` (no error), so the code thought it succeeded, closed the form, and re-fetched the unchanged data.

2. **"Still same bug" after my fix** — the dev server was already running when you added `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` to `.env.local`. Next.js only reads env vars at startup, so the key was `undefined` the whole time. The API route was making Supabase calls with an undefined key, failing silently again.

**Current state:** I restarted the server and tested the API route with a real listing ID — it saved `stock_qty: 99` to Supabase and I confirmed in the DB. The API route is working.

**What you need to do now:** Hard refresh the browser (`Ctrl+Shift+R` on Windows/Linux, `Cmd+Shift+R` on Mac) so the browser picks up the new JS code, then test the edit again.

> DEVELOPER

just tell me what the fix that i asked you

> AGENT

You asked: **farmer edits a listing → consumer page shows updated data**.

The fix has three parts, all done:

1. `fetchCache = 'force-no-store'` on both `/api/produce` routes — stops Next.js from caching Supabase responses server-side
2. `visibilitychange` listener on the consumer page — re-fetches when the user switches back to the tab
3. Server-side `/api/farmer/update-listing` route using the service role key — so the edit actually saves to the DB (RLS was silently blocking client-side updates)

All three are in place. Hard refresh the browser and test.

> AGENT

Found it. Two real bugs:

**Bug 1 — Manage modal doesn't reflect the save:** `load()` IS called after save, but the ProduceCard in the manage list re-uses the same React component instance (same `key={item.id}`). The data updates but `liveStock` is local state initialized only once at mount — it never re-syncs when the prop changes.

**Bug 2 — Consumer page:** Same problem: `liveStock` is `useState(item.stock_qty)` — only initialized at mount. Even if `fetchData` re-fetches fresh data and `available` updates, the `ProduceCard` component is updated (not remounted) because the `key` stays the same, so `liveStock` stays stale forever.

Fixing both now — `liveStock` needs a `useEffect` to stay in sync with `item.stock_qty`, and the manage modal needs an optimistic update so it doesn't depend on the re-fetch timing:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01W7wZ5MVbUJM57AAsmz5ciz
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/consumer/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)\n  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')\n  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)",
  "new_string": "  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)\n  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')\n  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)\n\n  useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])"
}
```

> AGENT

Clean. Here's what was actually wrong and what's fixed:

**The real bug:** `ProduceCard` stores `liveStock` in local React state initialized once at mount: `useState(item.stock_qty)`. When a farmer edits a listing and the consumer page re-fetches, React UPDATES the existing `ProduceCard` (same `key={item.id}`) instead of remounting it. So the price updates (it reads directly from props) but `liveStock` stays frozen at its original value forever — making it look like nothing changed.

**Fix 1:** Added `useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])` — keeps local state in sync when the prop changes.

**Fix 2:** The manage modal now does an **optimistic update** — the moment you click Save, it immediately patches that row in the list with the new values, so you don't have to wait for `load()` to return from Supabase.

Test it now: edit a listing, change price and qty, save — the manage modal should instantly show new values, and the consumer page should reflect them on next load.

Server is running again. Now try the Onion edit — change qty to 500, click Save Changes. If the server stops again you'll see "Network error" in the form instead of a silent hang.

The core problem was the WSL background process dying between sessions. Every time you close the terminal in WSL, background jobs get killed. To avoid this, run the dev server in the terminal you keep open, not as a background job — or keep the terminal session alive.

> AGENT

Server is up and clean. Both `/farmer/dashboard` and `/consumer` return 200.

**Why this keeps happening:** WSL kills background processes when terminals close. The old Next.js process stays alive on port 3000 as a zombie, and when you start again it picks 3001 with a corrupt `.next` state.

**Permanent fix — run the server yourself in a terminal you keep open:**
```bash
! npm run dev
```

Type that with the `!` prefix so it runs in this session and you can see the output directly. Don't run it in the background. Keep that terminal open while you work.

Now go test the Onion edit — change qty to 500, click Save Changes. You should see it update instantly in the manage modal, and the consumer page will show the new value on next load.
